- [ ] Set ScreenGui.ScreenInsets to DeviceSafeInsets for HUD elements that should sit at the edge.
- [ ] Test layouts in Device Emulator with Actual Resolution across Phone, Tablet, Desktop and Console.
- [ ] Re-check touch button overlaps in Studio Emulator or on a physical device after the April 2026 touch update.
- [ ] Set ResetOnSpawn deliberately per ScreenGui: true for health-style HUD, false for menus and persistent progress.
- [ ] Clamp bar ratios to 0-1 with UDim2.fromScale and tween changes instead of snapping.
- [ ] Clone a hidden template per row or item and sort with LayoutOrder.
- [ ] Set a fixed-size, scale-based corner radius on frames and buttons and set CornerRadius before per-corner overrides.
- [ ] Add UIShadow or a subtle semi-transparent stroke for depth; avoid heavy borders.
- [ ] Add toggles for camera shake, bobbing, particles, animation and brightness.
- [ ] Add a reduced-motion branch using the @ReducedMotionEnabledTrue style query.
- [ ] Blur the camera on menu open, store the default blur value and restore it on close.
- [ ] Hide the buy button once the player owns the product or all tiers.
- [ ] Restyle any free template before release and replace placeholder emojis with real art.
- [ ] Set CanQuery true on parts that carry interactive SurfaceGuis.
- [ ] Set DisplayDistanceType and MaxDistance on BillboardGuis to limit overhead clutter.
- [ ] Set ElasticBehavior to Never on touch scrolling frames where overscroll is unwanted.
- [ ] Pass true as the third BindAction argument for actions needing a touch button.
- [ ] Keep reserved keys (Esc, F9, F11, F12, PrintScreen) out of rebinds.
- [ ] Use TextChannelWindow for custom chat after disabling default chat windows.
- [ ] Keep per-frame GUI updates light and check MicroProfiler for dynamic GUI cost.
- [ ] Use Activated for all cross-platform button logic and SecondaryActivated for secondary actions.
- [ ] Clear the chat input box after sending a message.
- [ ] Test UI scale on phone, tablet and monitor sizes early, before building further layers (about 5 hours were lost fixing scale late).
- [ ] Use Scale-only sizing for frames and text, and set TextScaled for labels and buttons.
- [ ] Set ResetOnSpawn to false on any ScreenGui whose state must survive respawn.
- [ ] Build menu category tabs as separate frames and toggle Visible so only one list shows.
- [ ] Give each main-flow button a clear label (Play, Select Mode, Shop).
- [ ] Show where vehicles, mounts or key items are kept, with a visible pickup prompt.
- [ ] Use auto-reload or one-button reload for consumable weapons.
- [ ] Show crafting materials in the inventory.
- [ ] Warn on low durability or ammo with HUD and sound cues.
- [ ] Show a Coming Soon sign for unfinished features.
- [ ] Give every NPC a useful role.
- [ ] Show exact ability numbers (hits, cooldown, scaling ratio) in descriptions.
- [ ] Allow gear upgrades without forcing an unequip.
- [ ] Add a background-music mute toggle.
- [ ] Provide a touch-friendly action to recover lost items.
- [ ] Attach reference screenshots to every UI item in the design doc.
- [ ] Park FPS or debug labels in a screen corner, right-aligned and slightly down.
- [ ] Smooth long-text scrolling with a smoothing module or equivalent.
- [ ] Bind text properties to state so only changed properties update.
- [ ] Keep Fusion UI in client scripts.
- [ ] Build one configurable button component with variant props.
- [ ] Preview components in a story harness before wiring them into the game.
- [ ] Re-enable missing Studio panels via right-click on the top bar if the layout is lost.
- [ ] Check the current Studio version for native text shadows, gradients, blur and animated image containers before adding stroke, duplicate-label or gradient-image workarounds.
- [ ] Test any new native text shadow or gradient on the smallest target device and in the lowest graphics setting used by your players.
- [ ] When several shops or vendors serve the same purpose, consider one central hub and add a sign or map pin to it.
- [ ] Compare the native blur against any existing BlurEffect used behind menus and keep whichever one meets your performance budget.
- [ ] Before building custom hotkey hint text per device, check whether the Input Action Label has shipped and whether it covers your controls.
- [ ] Route all on-screen control hints through one component keyed by action name so the hint source can be swapped later.
- [ ] Before adding menu blur, UI shadows, gradients or text shadows as workarounds, check current release notes for native support.
- [ ] For chat moderation UI, use contextual real-time nudges and log whether they reduce reports in your own game.
- [ ] If players sell multiple items, add a sell-all option and an inventory selection menu so bulk selling is one step.
- [ ] If sales show repeated error messages per item, fix the flow to batch selections rather than adding more error text.
- [ ] If base walk speed is low (creator cites 20), add a sprint button for touch and a bindable sprint input for gamepad and PC.
- [ ] Wire the sprint input through the existing rebind rules; do not hardcode the key or reserve default inputs.
- [ ] When asking an AI assistant to change UI, name the exact script, frame or side to edit.
- [ ] After an AI assistant edits UI, ask it to verify its own changes and then test in Studio.
- [ ] Consider a UI pack as a starting point for consistent components when prompting AI.
- [ ] Clone currency flyouts from a hidden template, offset their start with scale-based spread, wait about 1 s, then tween to the HUD.
- [ ] Reset the flyout target position before each tween so window resizing doesn't break it.
- [ ] Shrink the flyout clone to size zero on arrival and pop the HUD counter.
- [ ] Hook HUD text and bars to the replicated value's .Changed event in a LocalScript instead of polling.
- [ ] Skip UICorner on progress fills that can get very small; test the smallest fill width.
- [ ] Set overhead BillboardGui StudsOffsetWorldSpace to about 0,3,0 and enable AlwaysOnTop.
- [ ] Use TextScaled labels with transparent backgrounds for floating overhead tags.
- [ ] Hide the dialogue box by default and toggle Enabled from the dialogue function.
- [ ] Enable click-to-skip on dialogue only after the typewriter finishes.
- [ ] Do not play click sounds when a TextBox is clicked (check IsA('TextBox')).
- [ ] Put item name in ProximityPrompt ActionText and price in ObjectText.
- [ ] Set ProximityPrompt HoldDuration to about 1 s and activation distance to about 20 studs.
- [ ] Disable a ProximityPrompt on the client once its location is unlocked.
- [ ] Early-return on gameProcessed in InputBegan so chat doesn't trigger game actions.
- [ ] Show exact numbers next to resource bars such as fuel.
- [ ] State buff and status-effect values numerically (e.g. heals X% of max HP).
- [ ] Announce level-ups visibly and list newly unlocked abilities.
- [ ] Remove options that have no effect from encounter or upgrade choices.
- [ ] On touch-only devices, set ScrollBarThickness to about 2% of parent AbsoluteSize.X.
- [ ] Icons: use an ImageLabel with AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5, scale-based size, BackgroundTransparency 1, the image set to the asset ID, and a UIAspectRatioConstraint.
- [ ] For a minimal visual system, pick one heavy sans-serif display font for headings (the source uses Montserrat ExtraBold) and keep body text in one family.
- [ ] Limit a new screen to text buttons, text labels and frames before adding extra element types.
- [ ] Choose a UI style that fits the genre before building the palette and components.
- [ ] For a shop, add a toggle button for the ScreenGui and a hidden product template per item.
- [ ] Wire purchase prompts to a visible button, not to a timer or an automatic trigger.
- [ ] Before release, audit every icon for all state variants (normal, darkened, disabled, owned).
- [ ] Confirm tutorial arrows do not overlap notifications or other HUD elements in testing.
- [ ] Add a low-graphics option (shadows off) and a music volume slider to settings.
- [ ] Rotate loading screen art from a set of images so returning players see variety.
- [ ] Add a reward or goal trail to quest UI so the objective shows what it pays.
- [ ] Check the game pass or purchase entry point is large and easy to find in the shop.
- [ ] Give quest buttons their own animation rather than a generic shared one.
- [ ] Show the contents of eggs and containers on their icons before opening.
- [ ] On join, read the current value of any shared state the HUD shows and set the label or bar before connecting to .Changed.
- [ ] Check that a newly joined player sees correct values without any change event firing.

## Where creators agree
- Roblox Creator Docs and Roblox DevForum both recommend Scale-first layouts over Offset (T4934, Q872, T9085).
- Roblox DevForum (2017), Roblox Creator Docs, 1Coal and janisjanis01 agree that UI should respect the device and not cover the game view (T4935, T4949, T9763, T5779).
- Roblox Creator Docs and Roblox DevForum agree on 9-slicing and spritesheets for image UI (T4945, T7744, T4946).
- Roblox Creator Docs and Roblox DevForum agree on contrast and stroke for text legibility (T4940, T4941, T9556).
- Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox DevForum, Duckable and RoDev agree on mocking or wireframing before building (T5502, T9201, T6109, T6461).
- janisjanis01 and Roblox Creator Docs agree on polished, animated UI feedback (T5817, T6567, T9575).
- Roblox Creator Docs and janisjanis01 both stress testing mobile input early (T6823, T7149, T9699).
- B Ricey and Roblox Creator Docs both value reusable UI components, with B Ricey using Roact templates and Docs using Layers and Components (T5379, T9725).
- Roblox Creator Docs and janisjanis01 agree on disabling default CoreGui and leaderboard features when replacing them (T8193, T5850).
- Size and position UI in Scale; reserve Offset for fixed pixel details such as padding and small icons (T10457, T12091, T14909, T15113, T15448, T13531).
- Use Activated for cross-platform button logic (T12401, T12462, T14244).
- Test on the Device Emulator or a physical device before release (T14115, T15191, T13281).
- Let layout objects own positioning instead of hand-placing children (T10686, T14911, T15115, T13536).
- Restyle templates rather than shipping them unchanged (T13643, T13762, T12228).
- Offer accessibility settings for motion, brightness, text size and colour (T11080, T11077, T11082, T14755).
- Use a clean sans-serif family consistently (T11768 Gotham, T14122 Montserrat).
- Decide ResetOnSpawn per ScreenGui: keep health-style HUD resetting, keep menus and progress persistent (T10946, T12320, T12337, T13530).
- Use a palette configured once and reused across the UI (T12226, T11765, T11124).
- Scale-based sizing is confirmed independently by Emancyphur (T15810), Possible Panda (T16298) and Poke (T16360).
- Clear, discoverable navigation and feature signposting is reinforced by Cumin (T16183, T16438) and RoyalDev (T16587, T16600).
- The official Roblox release and GamesBeat both report native blur and text shadows as new UI/2D features in 2026-09, so these features are real and recent.
- GamesBeat's gradient and blur mentions and the official 2D tools update point the same way: more built-in visual effects are available without custom assets.
- Size bars and spread effects in Scale, not Offset (PineappleDev, AlvinBlox, SimTek).
- HUD values should update from replicated values through .Changed (Suphi, SimTek).
- Short tween feedback on counters and buttons makes actions feel responsive (PineappleDev, Cumin).
- Keep features and UI simple: GameMakers (T18500) and VuukStudios (T18645) both favour simple features and a few UI primitives.

## Where creators disagree
- **TextScaled**
  - Roblox DevForum (2017) says avoid TextScaled blindly, because same-group buttons can end up with different text sizes (T4939).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) uses TextScaled inside Frames for SurfaceGui countdowns, overlays and signs (T7979, T10303, T9979), and says to scale text per device and test (T9557).
  - Prefer the Creator Docs for mechanics; keep T4939's group-consistency check.
- **Text outline method**
  - Roblox DevForum (2017) uses TextStrokeColor3 and TextStrokeTransparency (T4941, N2218).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) says a UIStroke child is the modern alternative and is preferred for stroke tweens (T9088, T7763).
  - Prefer UIStroke, but avoid tweening its Thickness on text (T7760).
- **Declarative vs imperative UI**
  - B Ricey (2021) recommends Roact with reusable templates, and warns that recreating elements on state change can hurt heavy UIs (T5379, T5380).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) says imperative UI is quicker to build and change, but declarative libraries (Roact, Fusion) make debugging easier as complexity grows (T9727).
  - This is a trade-off, not a contradiction: pick by project complexity.
- **Theme colour direction**
  - Roblox DevForum (2021) says do not always use dark themes; light themes suit cartoony UI (T5503).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) says prioritise legibility and use colours readable over both light and dark 3D backgrounds (T9547).
- **Device emulator vs real hardware**
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) recommends the Device Emulator and ViewportDisplaySize presets (T7432, T9379).
  - The same docs say to test touch targets and thumbstick reach on real phones, not emulated resolutions (T9699).
- **Mobile share**
  - Roblox Creators (2022, OFFICIAL) states ~66-67% of players are on mobile (T5707, N2411).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) says a large percentage of sessions are on PC/laptop, without a number (T7753). Design for mobile and PC equally.
- **Sizing with Offset versus Scale**
  - Older DevForum posts recommend Offset for sizes such as ScrollingFrame and health bars (T10456, T10457, T10580, 2017-2019).
  - 2024-2026 posts and official docs recommend Scale for size and position (T12091, T12225, T12461, T14909, T15113).
- **Click handler event**
  - T13535 (2026) says MouseButton1Click fires on both mouse click and phone tap.
  - T12462 (2024) and T14244 (official 2026) recommend Activated; T10695 (2020) recommended InputBegan/InputEnded/MouseButton1Up.
- **Detecting device type for layout**
  - T12570 (2025) hooks LastInputTypeChanged to show or hide mobile buttons dynamically.
  - T14349 (official 2026) says to avoid GetLastInputType for layout and use PreferredInput (T14346, T14348).
- **Hover handling**
  - T10695 (2020) says MouseEnter and MouseLeave often fail to fire; use InputBegan/InputEnded.
  - T12231 (2024) recommends MouseEnter hover tweens on buttons.
- **Borders and strokes**
  - T11112 (2021) says remove unnecessary borders because UIs look cleaner without them.
  - T11354 (2022) and T11774 (2023) add white or subtle strokes to define frames and containers.
- **Depth and shadow technique**
  - T11773 (2023) builds shadows from UIStroke transparency sequences or offset frames.
  - T13393 (2026) recommends UIShadow, which does not affect layout; prefer it.
- **Text sizing**
  - T12590 (2025, low-advice) suggests Text+ scaling for identical fit across devices.
  - T13534 (2026), T14122 and T14529 (official) use TextScaled with a consistent font.
- **Starting UI from templates**
  - T13643 (2026) says start from a free template to save time, then restyle.
  - T13762 (2026) says rebuild rather than copy-paste free UI packs.
- **Persisting GUI across respawn**
  - T10946 (2021, low-advice) enables ResetOnSpawn on the health ScreenGui so it recreates per spawn.
  - T12320, T12337 and T13530 (2024-2026) disable it to keep progress or menu state.
- **Touch detection: TouchEnabled versus PreferredInput**
  - Existing knowledge file rule: detect input with PreferredInput, not TouchEnabled.
  - SimTek Game Development (2025-09, T18293): gates the on-screen sprint button with UserInputService.TouchEnabled.
- **Un-hover tween timing**
  - PineappleDev (N7796): un-hover is about 0.5 s and described as a faster shrink-back than the 0.3 s hover.
  - Internal inconsistency in the source: a longer duration is not faster. Verify both values in Studio.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- Roblox Creator Docs items (2026-10) are the most current and are treated as official. Newer built-in systems include StyleSheet/StyleLink, tokens, query selectors, InputActionLabel (beta), gradients and upgraded UIShadow (beta, enable via File > Beta Features), and ModalManager/FeaturePackagesCore APIs. Verify availability before relying on them.
- 2015-2019 DevForum tips (ScaleType, TextStroke, TextScaled, Gotham font) predate the Constraint, UIStroke and StyleSheet tools. Prefer the 2026 Creator Docs for implementation.
- B Ricey (2021-01) Roact advice and 2024 items are older. Creator Docs (2026-10) cites Roact and Fusion as declarative options without ranking them.
- 2021 DevForum claims that Studio cannot upload font files and that blur only works on 3D elements may be outdated; verify current font upload and effects support.
- janisjanis01 (2026) and Duckable (2025-2026) reflect recent practice. Duckable's 2025 Figma and mood-board approach is current.
- The Feature Packages Core v5 hover change (0.5 -> 0.3 s) is version-specific; check the current package version.
- The platform mobile share figure (66-67%) is from 2022; check current platform stats before quoting.
- Crusherfire's AI-built UI effort and time savings are self-reported, unverified claims.
- Pre-2021 advice (Offset sizing, UICorner shader cost, MouseEnter/MouseLeave reliability, UIPageLayout quirks) is dated; prefer 2024-2026 Scale and Activated guidance.
- Official docs from October 2026 add PreferredInput, style queries, ScreenInsets, TextChannelWindow and StyleSheets transitions; prefer them over older TouchEnabled or GetLastInputType checks.
- The April 2026 touch button update moved default controls into ~60px side zones; re-verify old custom mobile layouts.
- UIShadow and per-corner UICorner radii (2026-05) supersede older UIStroke shadow tricks and CornerRadius-only styling; avoid mixing CornerRadius with per-corner values.
- Custom keybinds: T11087 (2021) said Roblox did not provide them out of the box; verify current support before relying on it.
- Shop access via MarketplaceService:OpenShop and SetCoreGuiEnabled(ExperienceShop) is from 2026-06; check it against current engine behaviour.
- Chat: with platform-integrated chat, custom message rendering is no longer supported (2026-10); older custom chat tutorials that read message text will break.
- Shift is bound to both Shift Lock and Sprint (T13208, 2026-02); verify current defaults before assuming conflicts.
- The newest items (2026-07 to 2026-10) cover Fusion and Charm state binding (Chase Higgins, Possible Panda). API details such as bind.effect and used-as may change with library versions; verify against the current release.
- Studio and ScreenGui advice from 2014 to 2020 (T15648, T15820, T15822) predates current Studio UI; verify it still holds.
- No new items contradict existing rules. Repeated TextScaled and Scale-sizing advice from 2020 and 2025 is consistent.
- Game-flow advice (RoyalDev 2026-08) is specific to certain game types; apply it selectively.
- 2026-09: Roblox officially listed new 2D tools with an orthographic camera, animated image containers, blur and text shadows (T17549). GamesBeat reports blur, text shadows and improved gradients (T17537). These are newer than the existing stroke-based legibility advice, so where a native text shadow is available, prefer it and treat stroke tricks as the fallback.
- The existing rule to blur the 3D world behind menus still applies. The new native blur may replace a BlurEffect workaround, but the sources do not say whether it applies to ScreenGui or only to 2D scenes.
- 2026-06: indie hub-consolidation tip (T17495) is self-reported, with no measured figures.
- As of 2026-11-01, the input action manager, Input Action Label, upgraded UI gradients, UI blur, UI shadows, text shadows and 2D particles are announced for late 2026/early 2027 (T17636, T17637, T17673). Treat them as upcoming and verify availability before relying on them.
- Once native UI blur, UI shadows, gradients and text shadows ship, existing workaround rules (such as blurring the 3D world with a separate effect, stroke-based depth, or duplicate-label text shadows) may be superseded. Prefer native features when they are available and confirmed.
- 2D capabilities (orthographic camera, sprite-sheet import) are also listed for late 2026/early 2027 (T17673) and are not yet confirmed as shipped.
- T17771 (Roblox Creator Docs, OFFICIAL, 2026-10): SetTeleportGui for custom teleport loading screens is a newer platform option; prefer it over any older approach that assumed the default teleport loading screen could not be changed.
- T17809 and T17810 are from 2026-08 and T17927 from 2026-10; all three are indie, self-reported advice. None conflicts with existing rules.
- Most recent items: Cumin (2026-06), Stewiepfing (2026-04, ProximityPrompt Custom style), SimTek (2025-09). Stewiepfing's Hologram module is third-party and may have changed.
- 2022 (Suphi) and 2024 (AlvinBlox, PineappleDev) items predate newer Roblox UI behaviour; verify property behaviour in current Studio.
- For touch detection, the existing PreferredInput guidance is kept as the default. The SimTek TouchEnabled approach is a simple gate, not a full hybrid-device check. No source here documents PreferredInput being deprecated.
- Items from 2024 and early 2025 are tagged low-advice by the creators' own framing; treat numbers as tutorial examples rather than benchmarks.
- T18409 (2019-12) and T18418 (2020-03) are older shop and purchase-prompt tutorials. Prefer newer guidance where it conflicts, and verify current purchase APIs before following them.
- T18512, T18500 and T18645 (2024 to 2026) and T18706 (2026-08) are the most recent items in this batch. Use them as the current view.
- Items come from janisjanis01 posts dated 2024-03 to 2025-10. Inventory and shop advice (2024-11) reflects a design change made after drag selection was removed for lag; prefer the newer multi-select approach over drag selection.
- No platform changes are stated in these sources; advice is indie self-reported and not verified against current Roblox docs.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If you lay out a new UI, then use Scale for size and position, add UIAspectRatioConstraint or RelativeYY for aspect, and cap pixel size with UISizeConstraint.
- If an interactive ScreenGui is placed, then keep it on CoreUISafeInsets. Use ScreenInsets None only for purely decorative backgrounds.
- If a control must be tappable on mobile, then keep it in thumb-reachable zones clear of the thumbstick and jump areas, and verify on a real phone, including TextBox focus and the on-screen keyboard.
- If a layout uses UIListLayout, UIGridLayout or UITableLayout, then never set Position or Size on the layout's siblings; use LayoutOrder and keep them as siblings.
- If a constraint and a UIListLayout target the same object, then expect the constraint to override the layout sizing; avoid combining them unintentionally.
- If text length is unknown or localised, then use AutomaticSize (and TextWrapped where wrapping is needed) rather than a fixed size, and test the longest language.
- If text must scale, then use TextScaled inside a Frame, keep MinTextSize at 9 or higher, and test on the lowest-supported device.
- If a SurfaceGui must be interactive, then place it in StarterGui with Adornee set to the target part; otherwise place a non-interactive SurfaceGui directly on the part.
- If a menu or modal opens, then give it a close button or back path, a DisplayOrder above the HUD, and hide the HUD while a Menu is open.
- If you tween UI, then reuse tweens per state, cancel running tweens first, avoid tweening UIStroke.Thickness on text, and fade whole groups with a CanvasGroup.
- If a layout must work on phones, read UserInputService.PreferredInput (not TouchEnabled) and listen for its change signal to swap layouts.
- If a button is added, connect Activated in a LocalScript under that button; avoid MouseButton1Click for new code.
- If placing custom mobile buttons, keep them about 60px from each side, out of bottom corners, and sized relative to JumpButton.
- If a frame or label is sized, use Scale; use Offset only for fixed padding, icon sizes or pixel details.
- If a bar or cooldown updates, clamp the ratio to 0-1, tween changes, and disconnect previous Heartbeat connections on re-trigger.
- If a ScreenGui should survive respawn, set ResetOnSpawn false; if it is a health HUD, leave it true.
- If building a shop or list, clone a hidden template per item and order it with LayoutOrder.
- If a feature is locked or owned, hide or swap its buy button instead of leaving it live.
- If the game needs accessibility, expose toggles for camera shake, bobbing, particles, brightness and text size, and respect reduced motion.
- If indie advice conflicts with newer official docs or 2026 properties, follow the official docs and say which source you chose.
- If a consumable weapon needs manual reload, switch to auto-reload or a single button (T16187).
- If a feature is unfinished, show a Coming Soon sign instead of an NPC or error message (T16397).
- If players report getting lost or not knowing what to do next, add labelled buttons and signage for the main flow (T16587, T16600, T16438).
- If a state change must update only one property, bind that property to its source instead of rebuilding the element (T16508).
- If a state change must trigger an effect, use bind.effect rather than click handlers (T16509).
- If building Fusion UI, keep it in client scripts to offload server work (T16295).
- If a component has many variants, expose one configurable component with variant props and raw-or-state inputs (T16322, T16325).
- If a UI scale issue appears, test across screen sizes before adding more layers (T16360).
- If a HUD element must survive respawn, set ResetOnSpawn to false (T15820).
- If low-advice items conflict with existing rules, keep the existing rule and treat the new item as a supplement.
- If a label needs legibility over a busy background and the current Studio build supports native text shadows, use the native shadow before adding strokes or duplicate labels.
- If an effect uses a native gradient, blur or animated image container, check it on mobile and at low graphics settings before treating it as ready.
- If a game has several vendors or shop booths that players or managers visit often, propose one central hub with a clear sign, and cite the walking-time reduction as a self-reported indie claim.
- If the 2D tools update is relevant to a UI task, verify the exact property names and Studio version in the official docs first, because the sources do not give them.
- If the target Studio or engine version is confirmed to include native UI blur, UI shadows, gradients or text shadows, use them over existing workarounds and note the change.
- If native support is not confirmed, keep existing workaround rules in place and do not assume the announced features exist.
- Do not state that the Input Action Label or input action manager is available unless verified in current docs.
- When designing HUD hints, reference actions by name so they can be bound to platform-provided labels later.
- Treat the chat-nudge figure as Roblox's own claim and not a guaranteed result for other games.
- If a task asks for a custom teleport or loading screen, use TeleportService:SetTeleportGui() on the client and build it as a ScreenGui with no scripts inside it.
- If a custom loading-screen ScreenGui needs behaviour, place the script in a separate client script, not inside the ScreenGui.
- If players must sell items one at a time and errors repeat, add sell-all and an inventory selection menu (T17809).
- If travel feels slow at the base walk speed, add a sprint control wired through existing input rules (T17810).
- When asked to change UI with an AI assistant, specify the exact target file or frame and require a self-check (T17927).
- Treat the 20 walk-speed figure as an example from one creator, not a platform default.
- If a HUD label or bar must reflect server state, connect a LocalScript to the replicated value's .Changed event instead of polling.
- If placing an overhead tag, set StudsOffsetWorldSpace near 0,3,0 and enable AlwaysOnTop so parts don't hide it.
- If a progress fill frame is very small, skip UICorner to avoid artifacts.
- If a dialogue line uses a typewriter, block click-to-skip until typing finishes.
- If a player clicks a TextBox, do not play the button click sound.
- If a ProximityPrompt's location is already unlocked, disable the prompt on the client.
- If InputBegan fires with gameProcessed true, return early and do not trigger game actions.
- If a resource bar or buff appears, show the exact number next to it.
- If a level-up or encounter outcome happens, show a visible message and list new abilities or effects.
- If you need to gate a touch-only button, prefer PreferredInput; use TouchEnabled only as a quick check and verify in Studio. For touch-only devices, set ScrollBarThickness to about 2% of the parent AbsoluteSize.X.
- If a request asks for a shop, build a toggled ScreenGui with product frames, and trigger purchase prompts from a button rather than automatically.
- If a request asks for a simple UI, use only text buttons, text labels and frames styled with UICorner, UIGradient and UIStroke.
- If a request names a genre, match the UI style to it: expressive for simulators, minimalistic for FPS.
- If a request needs an icon, use an ImageLabel with AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5, scale sizing, a transparent background and a UIAspectRatioConstraint.
- If a source is from 2019 or 2020, check it against current engine and purchase APIs before reusing its code.
- If a shop or inventory has many thin categories, merge them before adding more tabs.
- If a bulk-action list is needed and drag selection lags, use click and hover multi-select instead.
- If a container or egg icon hides its contents, show the item or pet on the icon.
- If a billboard notification blocks the view, remove it or move it to screen UI.
- If a tutorial arrow sits near notifications, test the overlap explicitly before release.
- If the game has a background track, provide a volume control as well as a mute button.
- If a UI element displays a shared or replicated value, set its initial text or size on join from the current value, then keep it updated with .Changed.
- If a health bar is built, use a clamped current/max ratio for its size; treat 500 as a tutorial figure, not a default, and check MaxHealth in the project.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No quantified UI performance benchmarks (e.g. ScreenGui cost per element, Roact re-render cost).
- Console-specific layout rules beyond TV focus and the Device Emulator presets are not covered.
- No current verification of 2021 claims: font upload limits, blur-effect limits, the DepthOfField tuning workaround.
- Localisation workflow details (translation tables, RTL languages, font coverage) are only briefly touched on.
- Accessibility settings players can toggle themselves (reduced motion, colour-blind modes, text scaling) are not described.
- Animation toolchains (Moon Animator details, Path2D and TweenService patterns) are only partly covered.
- Chat customisation beyond the listed config and ChatWindowConfiguration properties is not covered.
- Revenue, CCU and visit claims from creators are not covered and are unverified.
- Input glyph assets and specific icon art guidelines are not covered.
- No cost-benefit or A/B data on UI changes; the sources are anecdotal beyond the official docs.
- [low-advice video] items were not verified against the current engine version.
- Camera tuning (weights, pitch clamps, occlusion modes, first-person weighting) is only lightly covered and not core UI.
- No MicroProfiler benchmarks or frame-time budgets for GUI cost beyond general advice.
- Console and TV conventions (controller glyph art, TV safe-area numbers, focus navigation order) are thin.
- WCAG or other numeric contrast thresholds are not given; only tool names (Adobe Color, Coolors) are mentioned.
- Localisation and right-to-left layout beyond LayoutOrder negation, and multi-script font support, are not covered.
- Text+ library details and its tradeoffs versus TextScaled are not documented in the sources.
- Inventory libraries such as Sling (T13439) and built-in inventory themes are mentioned but not evaluated.
- StyleSheets full syntax, selector precedence and rule tooling are only partly sourced.
- UI sound design beyond a few one-line tips (asset preloading, Activated sounds) is not covered.
- Performance of UIShadow and UICorner on low-end mobile hardware is unverified.
- Shop access via MarketplaceService:OpenShop, debounced reveal clicks, package UI configure() and viewport model tips were dropped from this merge for space; re-add from the source notes if needed.
- No official Roblox documentation in these sources; all items are indie and self-reported, with no OFFICIAL items.
- No code examples or version details for Fusion or Charm APIs (used-as, bind.effect, computed).
- No concrete mobile touch-target sizes, text contrast ratios or minimum font sizes.
- No details on implementing console or gamepad selection layers (T15724 mentions a separate layer without specifics).
- No playtest data showing how reload, warning or signage changes affect retention.
- The smoothing module for scrolling frames (T15747) is not named or linked.
- Implementation of semi-transparent frosted-glass buttons (T16757) is not detailed.
- Audio accessibility beyond background-music mute (sound effects, subtitles) is not covered.
- Store thumbnail plugins (T16769) fall outside in-game UI design and are not included.
- Exact property names, Studio version and availability for native text shadows, gradients, blur and animated image containers are not in these sources. Check the official UI docs or release notes.
- It is unclear whether the native blur applies to ScreenGui/UI or only to the 2D scene tools. Verify against the docs.
- The orthographic camera from the 2D tools update is relevant to 2D game creation, not to UI layout in this topic. Research separately if needed.
- No performance cost data is provided for the new gradients, blur, text shadows or animated image containers on mobile or console.
- The shop-hub claim (T17495) has no measured numbers, only a self-reported reduction in walking time. Look for playtest data or designer case studies before making it a firm rule.
- API names, properties and usage for the input action manager and Input Action Label are not covered.
- Exact ship dates for native UI blur, UI shadows, upgraded gradients, UI-level 2D particles, orthographic camera and sprite-sheet import are not confirmed.
- Whether UI blur applies to ScreenGui or only to the 3D world is not covered.
- Whether nudge systems apply to text chat as well as voice chat is not covered.
- Performance costs of native UI blur, shadows and 2D particles on low-end mobile devices are not covered.
- T17771 does not say when during the teleport SetTeleportGui must be called, how long the custom GUI persists, or how it interacts with the default loading screen timing.
- No guidance on accessibility, mobile safe insets, or aspect ratio for teleport loading screens.
- No details on whether the custom GUI can be animated or whether it can display progress.
- Sprint implementation details: speed multiplier, stamina, and animation are not covered.
- Sell-all confirmation UX, error-message design, and undo behaviour are not covered.
- Specifics of prompting AI for Roblox UI and which UI packs to use are not covered.
- No official Roblox documentation was cited for these items; verify with the Creator Hub before relying on them.
- Camera control details (Scriptable versus Custom camera Type, 5 studs up and 15 back offset from T18343 and N7838) are camera topic rather than UI; research in the camera or scripting notes.
- Lobby decoration and shop economy (T18063) are level design, not UI.
- The Hologram module for ProximityPrompt Style Custom: source, API, maintenance status and licence are not documented here.
- The exact BillboardGui UDim2 size is ambiguous across the tip (4,0.20) and note (4,0,20); verify before using.
- No official Roblox documentation is cited in these sources. Verify ProximityPrompt Style, PreferredInput, and StudsOffsetWorldSpace behaviour against current docs.
- No per-frame tween performance numbers or mobile scrollbar tests beyond the 2% figure.
- Teleport transition clarity (T18194) overlaps existing SetTeleportGui guidance; check the teleport topic for current patterns.
- No official (OFFICIAL) pages were supplied in this batch, so the icon, shop and purchase-prompt recipes are unverified against current Roblox docs.
- No accessibility, mobile or console testing data for the shop or minimalist-style advice.
- No guidance on UI template licensing, pricing or quality checks before buying a pack.
- No font legibility or size data for heavy display fonts such as Montserrat ExtraBold.
- No monetisation policy or compliance guidance for purchase-prompt flows.
- No official Roblox guidance for loading screen image rotation or text-effects modules; verify against current docs and engine APIs.
- No measured performance numbers for drag-selection lag or per-frame cost of animated shop signs.
- No data on how many players used the low-graphics setting or whether it changed retention on mobile.
- No benchmarks for shop category counts or button sizes that improve findability.
- No coverage of tutorial arrow positioning standards (Scale, safe insets) beyond the overlap check.
- The source notes do not give the exact max-width scale values used for the BillboardGui health bar.
- No sources cover how to handle initial state for UI bound to attributes or Fusion state on join.
- In-world notepad, computer and book UI (T18997) is not detailed beyond the existing diegetic UI rule; check the existing creator notes for layout and Adornee specifics.

## Quotes (near-verbatim)
- "I make my UIs 100% in Scale unless I have a specific need for offset. All three of my recent games have had only a single UI that scales perfectly across all devices." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461))
- "Offset is not very good either, and for support on multiple devices will require redesigning your UI multiple times." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461))
- "Anything on the player's screen should only be there to inform the Player about things that they need to know that relate to the game." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258))
- "Some of my games don't have any button objects at all, it's all just Frames, TextLabels and ImageLabels." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948))
- "It's just not worth trying to mess around with an object that doesn't quite get exactly what you want where you could just make it yourself." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948))
- "Marking your entire game with an epilepsy warning means that people with epilepsy will be forced to choose between their safety and trying to play your game anyway." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919))
- "A golden rule of design is to never put pure white text on black backgrounds and vice versa." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805))
- "UI that has consistency throughout tends to be received better and appears better." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805))
- "The reason I feel this is appealing and clean is because it's simple and minimalistic." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083))
- "Such as UI corners so there are no icky corners." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083))
- "make sure that if you were a new player that you could easily understand what is going on" ([ByteBlox 2024-07 16:06](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=966))
- "A lot of Roblox games seemingly don't have progress bars; in something like an obby or tycoon a progress bar would do wonders." ([ByteBlox 2024-09 27:28](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=1648))
- "please disable the force field of the spawn location so many games they have the little force field that appears when you load in and that breaks immersion" ([GnomeCode 2024-10 10:30](https://youtu.be/H9CbgCe47Mo?t=630))
- "Roblox never tells you to use scale; it lets you do whatever, and a lot of people don't test on phone." ([ByteBlox 2024-11 14:10](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=850))
- "every time you join the game it'll pick a random background out of 11 Unique Images" ([janisjanis01 2024-11 21:12](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=1272))

## Official 2026 engine and API changes

_Chunk 12 (2026-11-01). Labels: OFFICIAL = Roblox Creator Docs release notes or staff DevForum posts. Status is as stated by the source (Pending = listed in release notes but not yet live). Items marked DEPRECATED name a deprecated, removed or sunset API/behaviour. Dates are post or release dates; 'Release N ~date' = Studio/engine release N, date approximate from the DevForum release-notes post._

- **Orthographic Camera Studio Beta** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-10-30): Adds Camera.ProjectionType (Perspective default, Orthographic), OrthographicSize (default 35), OrthographicMode, and ViewStretch. Studio-only; published games can't use it yet. Build impact: Experiment in Studio only; don't rely on it in published experiences yet. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4966772).
- **Input Action Manager fully released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-29): Window > Input > Input Action Manager adds a live State column in PlaySolo, UIModifier support, property editing, and undo/redo. Known issue: editing tools in play mode are unintended and will be removed. Build impact: Use it to author InputContexts, InputActions and bindings visually; don't depend on play-mode editing. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4965021).
- **UIShadow upgrades (beta)** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-10-14): Adds text shadows (Mode set to Text), inset shadows (Inset), knockout shadows (ShowBehindParent false), and gradient shadows via child UIGradient. Build impact: Cannot publish live experiences using these features during beta. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4934553).
- **TextChatService platform-integrated chat migration** (OFFICIAL, Test, 2026-10-21): TextChatService.PlatformIntegratedChat can be set to Enabled in Studio to test native chat. Required migration is May 2027; message content will no longer be accessible in code. Build impact: Test now; custom chat features relying on message content will break after the May 2027 migration. **DEPRECATED:** Custom chat features and code access to message content after the May 2027 migration Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4948433).
- **TextChatService role badges replace prefixes** (OFFICIAL, Pending, 2026-10-22): Role badges replace prefixes in February 2027. Badges can be set, removed, and read at runtime with a chosen color, and pass the text filter. Gradients come in a later update. Build impact: Plan to migrate prefix-based role displays to badges. **DEPRECATED:** Prefixes Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4948433).
- **TextChannelWindow in SurfaceGuis and read-only channels** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-22): TextChannelWindow works in SurfaceGuis. DisplaySystemMessage works in TextChannelWindow and supports rich text. TextSource.CanSend set to false creates read-only channels. Build impact: Enables in-world chat displays and announcement-only channels. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4948433).
- **GuiObject.InputSink replaces Active** (OFFICIAL, Deprecation, 2026-09-14): Adds GuiObject.InputSink for UI input handling, allowing the deprecation of GuiObject.Active. Build impact: Prefer InputSink in new UI; plan to migrate existing Active usage. **DEPRECATED:** GuiObject.Active Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4734).
- **UIGradient Type, TileMode and Scale** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-02): UIGradient adds Type (Linear, Radial, Conical, Elliptical), TileMode (Clamp, Repeat, Mirror), and Scale, and supports Path2D. Known issue: darker gradients show banding regressions, fix pending. Build impact: Watch for banding on dark gradients until the fix ships. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4846594).
- **MicroGamepad preferred input** (OFFICIAL, Full release, Release 739 ~2026-09-17): UserInputService.PreferredInput can be set to Enum.PreferredInput.MicroGamepad for gamepads without a thumbstick, such as TV remotes. Build impact: Support thumbstick-less navigation for TV-remote style devices. Source: Creator Docs release notes 739.
- **ClipsDescendants supports rotation** (OFFICIAL, Deprecation, 2026-07-14): StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation lets parent ClipsDescendants clip rotated GuiObjects and Path2D. Enabled by default from 2026-09-24 (Phase 2); property removed in Phase 3 (November 2026). Build impact: Check rotated UI inside clipping parents; the new behavior will apply to all games. **DEPRECATED:** StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation (removed in Phase 3, November 2026) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4737864).
- **InputActionLabel and binding display APIs** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-08-06): InputActionLabel is a GuiObject displaying an InputAction's preferred binding; set its InputAction property. Read-only ResolvedText and ResolvedImageContent show the result. InputAction.PreferredBinding and InputBinding DisplayName/DisplayImage are live. Build impact: Use for per-device control prompts instead of hard-coded key text. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4779420).
- **InputAction.DisplayName** (OFFICIAL, Full release, Release 740 ~2026-09-22): Adds InputAction.DisplayName to provide a localized display name for Input Actions to be used in UI. Build impact: Use it for localized action names in UI. Source: Creator Docs release notes 740.
- **Server Authority breaking change: camera** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-07-09): With Server Authority fully released, Camera InputAction stops synchronizing. Use Player:GetCameraState(), returning a table with CFrame, FieldOfView, and ViewportSize synced from client to server. Build impact: Migrate server camera reads to Player:GetCameraState(). **DEPRECATED:** Player.InputContexts.CameraContext.CameraAction; Camera InputAction replication Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4727993).
- **PlayerScripts IAS update** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-04-30): PlayerScripts UseInputActionSystem updated: some actions renamed, camera actions separated into their own contexts, and context priorities changed. Build impact: Re-check custom code referencing default action names or context priorities. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4606949).
- **Default touch control positions changed** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-30): Default positions and sizes of the touch thumbstick and jump button changed on all touch devices via default player scripts. Build impact: Check custom UI for overlap with the new positions. **DEPRECATED:** Previous default touch thumbstick and jump button positions Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4565267).
- **StyleQuery fully released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-11): StyleQuery adds layout conditions (MinSize, MaxSize, AspectRatioRange), global queries (ViewportDisplaySize, PreferredInput, ReducedMotionEnabled, PreferredTextSize) and @ selectors in StyleRules, including @PreferredTextSizeMedium through Largest. Build impact: Build responsive and accessible UI styling without scripts. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4566519).
- **UICorner per-corner radii** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-14): UICorner supports TopLeftRadius, TopRightRadius, BottomLeftRadius and BottomRightRadius. CornerRadius remains as an alias setting all four corners. Build impact: Existing CornerRadius usage keeps working. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4636263).
- **UIShadow released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-06-23): UIShadow is a native 2D drop shadow with blur, color, transparency, offset and spread, plus UIShadow.Enabled. Publishing no longer requires the New UI Capabilities beta flag. Build impact: Use UIShadow instead of image-based shadows. **DEPRECATED:** New UI Capabilities Studio Beta flag removed Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4636263).
- **Instance.QueryDescendants** (OFFICIAL, Full release, Release 697): Adds an Instance.QueryDescendants method for descendant lookup using a query selector string, similar to selectors in UI styling. Build impact: Use selector strings to find UI descendants concisely. Source: Creator Docs release notes 697.
- **Device Simulator toolbar and multi-touch simulation** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-09): Device Simulator gains a top-of-viewport toolbar (Test > Device Simulator): switch devices during playtest, auto-match DPI, show touch hints. Multi-touch Simulation is fully released with customizable pinch/rotate shortcuts. Build impact: Test touch UI across devices in Studio. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4861695).
- **Color3 contrast helpers** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 745 ~2026-10-27): Adds Color3:GetContrastingColor(), Color3:GetContrastRatio(), and Color3:GetLuminance() for choosing legible, WCAG-contrasting colors. Build impact: Use for accessible text and background colors. Source: Creator Docs release notes 745.

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2014-12-27 Emancyphur [0:59](https://youtu.be/AFY6Yfm-oaw?t=59): Enable Explorer, Properties, Toolbox, Game, Advanced and Command panels via right-click on empty space in Studio's top bar.
- 2015-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313): For resizable GUI borders, set ImageLabel/ImageButton ScaleType to Slice so the image is sliced instead of stretched.
- 2015-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313): SliceCenter is Rect2D (X0,Y0,X1,Y1); the two points define the center box. Everything outside it is border and follows 9-slice rules.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Use Offset only (no Scale) for elements that must not stretch, such as health bars, and keep a fixed Offset padding (e.g. 10) from screen edges.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Use Scale for position at center or edges (e.g. X Scale 0.5 for top center) and Offset for size, so UI doesn't stretch across different screens.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Keep UI grid-based with one universal padding value applied across all frames for consistent alignment.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Structure windows like websites: header for title/categories, body for items, footer for currency/resources; drop the footer when information is minimal.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Use concept art or reference games to decide layout, since one interface design does not fit all games.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Use color for meaning: red for danger/health, green for good/health/money, yellow for money/gold, black and white for foreground/background.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Use contrast in color weight (dark header and footer, lighter body) to create visual gravity and structure.
- 2017-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526): Place gradients and bright, pure colors on the most important information to act as a focal point; use gradients on selected category icons to show current state.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Build UIs mainly in Scale rather than Offset; use Offset only when pixel-exact sizing is specifically needed.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Design for the device aspect ratio, not just Studio's screen size; Studio layouts often look different in-game on other devices.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use SizeConstraint RelativeYY to keep aspect ratio; size relative to the dominant axis (e.g. width 2x height = {2,0},{1,0} for 2:1).
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use RelativeXX for objects inside a vertical ScrollingFrame so they don't grow as CanvasSize increases; RelativeYY for horizontal ones.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use AnchorPoint (e.g. 0,1 with Position Y scale 1) to pin elements to screen edges without extra position math.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Avoid TextScaled blindly; check visual consistency so buttons in the same group don't end up with different text sizes.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use white or bright text by default; for dark colours like blue or red, lighten them or add a stroke or background.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Give text a TextStrokeColor3 that contrasts with the text colour; white text can use stroke transparency ~0.8 for subtle separation.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Keep the background behind text simple and uncluttered so letters remain readable.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Avoid text wrapping in buttons; resize the button, shrink the text, or disable TextWrapped for consistent appearance.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Do not display images larger than their original resolution; it causes pixelation and blur.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use 9-slicing (ScaleType Slice, SliceCenter in pixels) so rounded or bordered images stretch without distortion.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use spritesheets with ImageRectSize and ImageRectOffset to swap hover or animation states without uploading many images.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Add padding between element edges and parent frames; use UIPadding or manual sizing, and separate text from frame edges with a child label.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Maintain contrast between elements and keep UI styling consistent; restyle free models or bought GUIs to match your game.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Keep UI from covering the game view; large sidebars covering a third of the screen waste space.
- 2017-09-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461): Use ZIndex within a ScreenGui for layering; use ScreenGui DisplayOrder to layer separate ScreenGuis.
- 2018-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677): Aim-assist arc previews help players target throws; update the beam every RenderStepped frame from the mouse hit position.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Study the UI Design Starter Guide and A Guide to User Interface Design threads; use Gui-Designer to drag and scale GUI instances.
- 2019-01-26 AlvinBlox [21:11](https://youtu.be/0UhMpxxRFyc?t=1271): Leaderboard stats must be a Folder named exactly 'leaderstats' (lowercase, one word) parented to the player, or the leaderboard will not show.
- 2019-03-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754): Give leaderboard rows gold/silver/bronze colours for places 1-3 and a header bar to make the board look appealing.
- 2019-03-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754): If a part blocks scrolling on a Surface GUI leaderboard, move the Surface GUI part forward so the ScrollingFrame sits above it.
- 2019-04-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895): Set ClipsDescendants to true on the spinner's border frame so crates stay clipped inside the spinner window.
- 2019-04-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895): Add a debounce and loading/error text on the spin button to prevent double-spinning and to show failed requests.
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Judge every UI concept against three principles: usable (responds to any interaction), navigable (no maze to find store or settings), and desirable (clean, no excess, fits game style).
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Build UI mockups in a vector tool like Adobe XD rather than directly in Studio, since Studio's built-in UI design tools are limited.
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Keep UI elements consistent in size and style across the whole interface by designing each element carefully against one shared spec.
- 2019-10-11 Last Level Studios [7:23](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=443): Design UI for mobile and console from the start; retrofitting Xbox selection onto a GUI-heavy game can require a separate selection layer.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Keep on-screen UI limited to information relevant to gameplay; avoid persistent promo text like 'buy our gamepasses' that obstructs the view.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Pick a UI style that fits the game's theme (e.g. dark, metal or concrete textures for a medieval RPG) rather than bland generic shapes.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Use a dark inset panel behind currency numbers so the text contrasts well against the textured frame.
- 2019-11-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792): Size ScrollingFrames with Offset rather than Scale, as the author recommends.
- 2019-11-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792): Use UI constraints (UIListLayout/UIGridLayout) to lay out scrolling content; the author says to use them if you aren't already.
- 2019-11-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792): Set ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize to the layout's AbsoluteContentSize X and Y so content neither overscrolls nor underscrolls.
- 2019-11-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792): For static content like a store GUI, set CanvasSize once manually or via script.
- 2019-12-01 TheDevKing [7:07](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=427): Trigger the purchase prompt from a button in a ScreenGui rather than automatically, so players choose to buy.
- 2019-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996): Build a radial bar from corner pieces: use one 500x500 TopLeft piece rotated via UIGradient for three quarters, mapping health to 1/3 segments.
- 2019-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996): Use NumberSequence keypoints 0.001 apart; equal times can be ordered randomly, so offset the fill edge to avoid errors.
- 2019-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996): Reposition keypoints when rotating a UIGradient, since rotation skews the edges of the frame; scale keypoints to match the rotation angle.
- 2020-02-07 Last Level Studios [20:08](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=1208): Use a scrolling-frame smoothing module for long-text UIs; default Roblox scrolling frames teleport and make reading annoying.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584): Tween UI frames on and off screen using TweenService with predefined On/Off UDim2 positions for battle transitions.
- 2020-03-12 TheDevKing [11:08](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=668): Build the shop as a ScreenGui with a toggle button; each product frame holds image, title, description and price labels.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Add a center-screen dot during testing; aim offset can look correct yet be visually misaligned.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [28:37](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1717): Use scale-based sizes and positions with AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5 so GUI scales across screen sizes; set TextScaled true.
- 2020-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791): Don't store animations inside the script output path only; put animation references in a settings module so missing animations don't throw errors.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use UICorner for rounded corners; a scale of 0.5+ on a square makes a circle. Heavy use can hurt performance since it is a shader applied over the rendered object.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use UIListLayout and UIGridLayout instead of scripting positions; for grid cells, parent a UIAspectRatioConstraint inside the UIGridLayout to keep aspect ratio.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): For simple menus, manually toggle Visible/Enabled rather than UIPageLayout, which often needs cut/paste to refresh after visibility or LayoutOrder changes.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use UIScale on every element that must resize with screen size; then only update each UIScale's Scale value instead of recalculating sizes individually (e.g. 0.7 for smaller GUIs).
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use UISizeConstraint MinSize to stop elements shrinking to unreadable sizes on small screens.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Anchor GUIs to screen edges (e.g. X position {1,0} with AnchorPoint) so elements never float unintentionally in the middle of the screen.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use the top 36px inset: set ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset to true or compensate with offsets; account for it in code via GuiService:GetGuiInset().
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use numeric rbxassetid:// IDs (Copy ID in Explorer), not rbxgameasset:// URLs, which break across games and when assets are renamed.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Add sound to UI interactions, including error sounds (e.g. insufficient funds) to communicate state to the player.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use GUI InputBegan/InputEnded/MouseButton1Up events for reliable hover and click handling; MouseEnter/MouseLeave often fail to fire.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Process a button press only if the mouse is released while still over the button, the standard real-world convention for a good experience.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Size a ScrollingFrame's CanvasSize by binding to layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal('AbsoluteContentSize') so scrolling always works correctly.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use camera ViewportSize for full screen size (no inset); use TextService:GetTextSize to measure text.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use TweenService to animate any GUI property, storing the Tween object for reused open/close animations and calling Play().
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): For ViewportFrames, set the camera CFrame to look at the model from outside it, lower FieldOfView below 70 for better 2D viewing, and reassign CurrentCamera via script at runtime.
- 2020-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948): Use WorldModel instead of Model in a ViewportFrame to enable physics and animation on characters.
- 2020-06-26 GnomeCode [30:31](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=1831): Add an in-world GUI (notepad, computer interface, book popup) to deliver story or interaction; it adds depth cheaply.
- 2020-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943): Add a settings menu to toggle each camera effect (bobbing, blur, sprint) on or off, since players have different preferences.
- 2020-07-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753): On mobile, the module does not handle input; bind your own UI buttons to raise, lower, rotate, terminate and requestPlacement, detected via getPlatform() returning "Mobile".
- 2020-08-19 Emancyphur [0:39](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=39): Set frame Size offset to 0 and use only Scale so the GUI scales across phone, tablet and monitor sizes.
- 2020-08-19 Emancyphur [0:39](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=39): Enable TextScaled on text labels and buttons so text resizes with the button across devices.
- 2020-08-19 Emancyphur [47:17](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2837): Disable ResetOnSpawn on the ScreenGui so the selected loadout label does not reset to default on respawn.
- 2020-08-19 Emancyphur [52:28](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=3148): Build category tabs as separate frames and toggle Visible so only one category list shows at a time.
- 2020-09-17 Emancyphur [4:16](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=256): Add accessibility options such as colorblind mode for puzzle games where players must distinguish similar colored items.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Set GUI Visible to false instead of tweening UI off-screen; hidden GUIs stop rendering and input.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Reduce tweens on GUI; dynamic GUI costs more draw time than static GUI, visible in MicroProfiler.
- 2020-12-11 SimTek Game Development [1:08](https://youtu.be/8QUJ_Qc6JTs?t=68): Use a UIGridLayout with relative CellSize (e.g. scale 0.4 X) and centered alignment so inventory slots distribute evenly.
- 2020-12-11 SimTek Game Development [2:30](https://youtu.be/8QUJ_Qc6JTs?t=150): Number inventory slot buttons so you can find the lowest empty slot in code when filling the bag.
- 2020-12-11 SimTek Game Development [3:13](https://youtu.be/8QUJ_Qc6JTs?t=193): Anchor quantity labels at (1,1) in the bottom-right of each slot; set size in scale so they fit any slot size.
- 2021-01-04 B Ricey [14:02](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=842): Use Roact (Roblox's React-style UI library) with reusable element templates so UI components can be reused without editing each one.
- 2021-01-04 B Ricey [15:23](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=923): In Roact, update UI by creating new element state rather than mutating instances; be aware the recreate cost can hurt performance on heavy UI.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [5:22](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=322): Use mouse.Hit.Position from the built-in Roblox mouse for aiming; a custom mouse module is optional.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705): Enable ResetOnSpawn on the health ScreenGui so it is recreated with each character spawn.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705): Drive bar width with UDim2.fromScale(health ratio, 1) and clamp the ratio to 0-1 to avoid overflow.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705): Connect to Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal for Health and MaxHealth so the bar updates on damage, healing and max changes.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705): Use TweenService to animate bar size changes for smooth feedback instead of snapping to new values.
- 2021-03-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877): Set Character.Archivable = true before cloning, then reset to false; Character:Clone() fails on non-archivable characters.
- 2021-05-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208): Position the viewmodel to the camera CFrame every frame inside RenderStepped so the arms track the view without lag.
- 2021-05-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507): Use a spring for bobble driven by player velocity and sway driven by mouse delta, applied to the viewmodel and HumanoidRootPart.
- 2021-05-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507): Tween an AimAlpha NumberValue (not IntegerValue) to 1 or 0 and lerp the sight toward the viewmodel camera for smooth aiming.
- 2021-05-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507): Scopes block the default mouse raycast; use a custom GetMouse raycast from the camera with RaycastParams to aim through scoped guns.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Sketch the UI roughly first (MS Paint is fine); avoid overdetailing so the sketch stays a quick layout plan.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Build a layout skeleton in Figma before details so buttons and text align without overlapping elements.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Don't always use dark themes; use light themes for cartoony or playful UI.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Experiment with rounded corners on frames and buttons depending on the UI style.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Use a palette tool like Coolors and lock colors you like while regenerating to build a scheme.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Set Anchor Point to 0.5,0.5 on background frames and position elements via the Properties tab rather than the UI editor, which is more precise.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Roblox Studio cannot upload font files; pick a similar built-in font (e.g. Gotham for Montserrat).
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Use an AutoScale plugin on UI elements so the interface scales across screen sizes.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Blur only works on 3D-space elements (decals, parts, SurfaceGuis); ScreenGui frames cannot be blurred directly.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Use a subframe named 'Blur' with scale offsets around -3 and Visible off, and place the LocalScript inside it to reduce edge bleeding.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Tune DepthOfField settings during playtesting to minimise bleeding around frame edges.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Very rounded frames (circles, non-square ImageFrames) do not work well with this effect; UICorner with offset around 5 to 7 still looks acceptable.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Tweening, scaling and positioning of blurred frames work without breaking the effect.
- 2021-07-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459): Build a loading bar as nested frames: a rounded base, a clipping frame set to clip descendants, and a Top frame sized {1,0,1,0} inside it.
- 2021-07-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459): Scale the bar by setting Clipping width to the ratio and Top width to 1/ratio so the inner fill stays correctly sized.
- 2021-07-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459): Darken the ImageBase color when building image-based bars so the difference between the two images is visible.
- 2021-07-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996): Use different weights for horizontal and vertical camera motion (e.g. WEIGHT_XZ=20, WEIGHT_Y=5) since screens are wider than tall.
- 2021-07-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996): Disable camera weight in first person by switching CameraSubject back to the Humanoid when the camera is within 1 stud of the head; weight otherwise blocks the screen.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Build contrast/saturation sliders with normal in the centre, lowest on the far left and highest on the far right, so lower contrast is available as well as higher.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Provide brightness sliders; dark scenes hide content for low-vision players while bright ones cause eyestrain or seizures. Don't rely on a single default light source.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Use Adobe's Color Accessibility tool to pick UI palettes safe for every colour-blindness type, so colourblind players don't need a special mode.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Offer a colour-lock style option that limits team/ink colours to maximally contrasting pairs, as Splatoon does, for colourblind players to tell allies from enemies.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Add toggles to disable camera shake, view bobbing, particles (minimal/decreased/all) and animation; reduce overstimulation, motion sickness and seizure risk, and lag.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Add a health/epilepsy warning before play if you cannot remove strobing or camera-shake effects, and avoid strobing lights where possible.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Provide size adjustment for cursor, text, chat box and GUI elements (scale, width, height) so low-vision and dyslexic players can read and track them.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Support customisable keybinds; ergonomic and RSI players often need non-default layouts, and Roblox does not yet provide this out of the box.
- 2021-07-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919): Offer toggle alternatives to held buttons (toggle sprint, crouch, fly, emotes) and avoid requiring holding multiple keys at once or repeated presses.
- 2021-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727): Set a GUI element's AnchorPoint to 0.5,0.5 before rescaling so it stays centred; drag it back to its original position afterward.
