- 2021-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727): Use a scaling plugin to rescale both Position and Size of selected UI so it fits different device resolutions.
- 2021-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727): Add UIAspectRatioConstraint to keep a frame's aspect ratio unchanged across devices; example 2:1 frame became 131x65 instead of distorted 131x90 on iPhone 4S.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Don't stretch text inputs to fill the full screen width; forms don't need to be 100% wide.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Use visible labels instead of placeholders; placeholders vanish while typing and hurt accessibility, though they're fine in some cases.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Reduce the number of required form fields so users can finish forms correctly on the first try.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Pre-fill form data when known, such as the player's username, so users don't type it manually.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Limit the characters an input accepts to prevent errors before submission.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Remove unnecessary borders; UIs look cleaner without them. Solo developers without a designer tend to overuse borders.
- 2021-08-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491): Increase clickable area: bigger targets are easier to hit. For small controls, add an invisible clickable area around them.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Hide unimportant text and secondary info behind buttons, links, or menus so the main UI stays uncluttered and easier to navigate.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Keep fonts, colors, spacing, and icon styles consistent throughout the interface; consistent UI is received better and appears more polished.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Structure element hierarchy so key information is prominent and minor items are de-emphasized, preventing players from missing information.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Use text weight and size to establish type hierarchy, ordering text by importance so the most important reads first.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Build contrast using value and saturation differences rather than just complementary hues; complementary colors alone can look poor together.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Ensure sufficient contrast for accessibility, since color vision deficiency affects many players.
- 2021-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805): Avoid pure white text on black backgrounds and pure black text on white backgrounds to reduce eye strain; use softer tones like dark gray.
- 2021-11-24 GnomeCode [5:47](https://youtu.be/myaGOnACzSY?t=347): Use a LocalScript under the button and connect Activated to handle clicks, since server scripts can't read a player's device input.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Lock the camera to first person only at the end of development; test in third person during building for convenience.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Add flavor text to items and make them more interactable, but keep monologue short so it does not become annoying.
- 2021-12-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301): Lighting filters (Sun rays, Color Correction, Bloom, Depth of Field) add mood; Color Correction is widely used in tycoons and simulators to make colors more vivid.
- 2022-01-04 GnomeCode [8:26](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=506): Build health bars as a BillboardGui with AlwaysOnTop enabled, reduce max/max-width scale, and drive the green bar's Size from current/max health in script.
- 2022-04-14 Suphi Kaner [50:14](https://youtu.be/FVOowr2DXbM?t=3014): Tween wheel orientation (about 0.4 s) instead of setting it instantly so steering looks smooth rather than snapping.
- 2022-04-14 Suphi Kaner [55:01](https://youtu.be/FVOowr2DXbM?t=3301): Use a ProximityPrompt on the seat with RequiresLineOfSight off, and disable it while occupied (watch Occupant changes).
- 2022-04-14 Suphi Kaner [59:54](https://youtu.be/FVOowr2DXbM?t=3594): On exit, teleport the player to the highest Y-axis attachment on the seat so they don't land under a flipped car or fall into gaps.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Use UICorner on frames and images (radius around 0,15) to avoid harsh corners; the author says rounded edges make the UI easier on the eyes.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Add a white UIStroke (thickness 3, ApplyStrokeMode Contextual) to the main frame to define its edges against the game background.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Use Interface Tools plugin to build UI quickly, and keep layouts simple and minimalistic for a clean look.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Place the title bar label at the top-middle, hanging slightly over the frame edge, so the UI's purpose is clear at a glance.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Build a close button as a small red TextLabel at the top-right with a UICorner (radius 0,8) and a white border stroke.
- 2022-04-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083): Tint a background image with a color (29,29,209) and transparency 0.7 to add subtle color without clutter.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [26:01](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1561): Test controls and layout on paper first. Around 66-67% of Roblox players are on mobile, and control design makes or breaks success.
- 2022-06-30 Suphi Kaner [30:30](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=1830): Drive HUD text from a server-replicated value by connecting to .Changed on the value in a LocalScript, so the UI updates live.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [5:01](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=301): Set the BindAction createTouchButton argument to true when you need a mobile touch button for the action; false otherwise.
- 2023-01-07 SimTek Game Development [11:23](https://youtu.be/zmTsh66uxsI?t=683): Clone a hidden template label per message and parent it to the same ScreenGui as the script, so messages display sequentially instead of all at once.
- 2023-01-07 SimTek Game Development [12:47](https://youtu.be/zmTsh66uxsI?t=767): Tween the label with TweenInfo: about 1s duration, Quad easing, Out direction, and a ~2s delay so players can read it before it moves off screen.
- 2023-01-07 SimTek Game Development [16:14](https://youtu.be/zmTsh66uxsI?t=974): Wait for Tween.Completed then Destroy the label to avoid cluttering the client GUI.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [9:27](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=567): Keep pitch angles small (clamp around ±45 or less); large pitch values look odd.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [10:41](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=641): Zoom via camera.FieldOfView: default 70; lower values zoom in (60), higher zoom out (80).
- 2023-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571): Add quality-of-life options: progress saving, subtitles, objective/puzzle hints, skippable cutscenes, brightness sliders, toggleable crouch/sprint.
- 2023-04-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-custom-chat-tag/2302562): Chat tags can be customised by editing the colored PrefixText string in the TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage callback; the script is meant to be modified.
- 2023-05-07 1Coal [2:41](https://youtu.be/JzB8fpyofnw?t=161): Replace text progress with a large progress bar and make side panels collapsible or smaller so gameplay fills the screen.
- 2023-05-07 1Coal [4:43](https://youtu.be/JzB8fpyofnw?t=283): Audit the game against successful front-page games and give the developer a detailed written change list.
- 2023-06-15 Suphi Kaner [1:16:01](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=4561): Align per-second HUD timers to whole seconds by waiting 1 minus (time % 1) so the display ticks cleanly.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Keep one constant color scheme across the UI; pick palettes with a color tool such as Adobe Color rather than ad hoc choices.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Make primary actions visually distinct from secondary ones: give the primary button a background color and remove the background from Cancel, so users don't misclick.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Place key navigation (e.g. Log In / Sign Up) in consistent, predictable locations so users never have to search for them.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Avoid over-expressive display fonts for primary text; use a clean font family (author recommends Gotham and its weights) throughout.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Check text contrast with a contrast checker (e.g. Coolors contrast checker); avoid low-contrast pairs like yellow on white.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Use flat, one-color vector icons (e.g. Google Material icon set, Font Awesome) instead of large multicolor icons.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Use gradients sparingly: as subtle shading, on highlighted text, or matched to a stroke on the primary button, never on all text.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Reduce clutter by using collapsible menus and dividers; for black backgrounds use slightly lighter dividers, for white backgrounds slightly darker ones.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Add depth with shadows: UIStroke with a transparency NumberSequence from 0 to 1 linearly, thickness ~2, black; or duplicated offset frames behind with lower ZIndex; or a drop-shadow texture placed behind the frame.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Use a subtle stroke on containers, slightly darker than the fill and partly transparent, to separate elements.
- 2023-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099): Use blur behind top bars or panels rather than a solid background for a cleaner look; a stronger blur often looks better.
- 2023-08-18 SimTek Game Development [12:15](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=735): Hide or disable the gamepass buy button once the player owns the pass to avoid confusing prompts.
- 2023-08-29 janisjanis01 [0:25](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=25): Add polished GUI animations and click sound effects to menus and buttons to make the game feel more finished.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Offer graphics presets (High/Medium/Low/Very low) that remove shadows, fog, clouds and effects for weaker devices.
- 2023-09-12 janisjanis01 [2:20](https://youtu.be/9aQpeTCZ7yM?t=140): Replace default Roblox leaderstats with a custom GUI showing wins; hide the default leaderboard by disabling its display.
- 2023-09-24 VuukStudios [2:54](https://youtu.be/dcJtZnan7i8?t=174): Use UIScale/UDim2 unit conversion (AutoScale plugin) on buttons so positions and sizes scale across phones and avoid overlapping controls on different devices.
- 2023-09-24 VuukStudios [1:21](https://youtu.be/dcJtZnan7i8?t=81): Make mobile buttons circular with a UICorner at 50 offset, semi-transparent gray background (~0.5), white text with stroke transparency 0.
- 2023-11-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585): Route all UI open/close through a single visibility manager keyed by screen position so overlapping frames close automatically; distinguish temporary frames from default HUD frames.
- 2023-11-26 Roox4 [3:14](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=194): Fill the lobby with decorations and a shop so it feels populated and gives players a reason to earn tokens.
- 2024-02-17 B Ricey [19:49](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=1189): Use ContextActionService:BindAction with a keycode for prototyping input before building a GUI, so UI design doesn't block placement work.
- 2024-03-08 GameMakers [6:18](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=378): Make a feature simple enough to explain to your grandmother; if it needs explaining, streamline it before shipping.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [12:03](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=723): Use TextChatMessageProperties with rich text markup in the Text property to recolor or restyle incoming chat messages.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [21:33](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=1293): Use TextChannel:DisplaySystemMessage on RBXGeneral to show server announcements in chat with custom formatting via string.format.
- 2024-03-24 Stewiepfing [10:48](https://youtu.be/zMH1q2IzFYA?t=648): Display an icon with an ImageLabel: anchor 0.5,0.5, scale size, background transparency 1, image set to the asset ID, plus a UIAspectRatioConstraint.
- 2024-03-29 janisjanis01 [4:50](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=290): Show the reward trail in a small frame on the quest UI so players see what they are working toward.
- 2024-03-29 janisjanis01 [5:51](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=351): Give quest buttons a separate animation script rather than reusing a generic one, which looked poor for those buttons.
- 2024-03-30 B Ricey [10:32](https://youtu.be/1XJRZ1x2x4g?t=632): Show a per-frame preview: tint the placement outline blue when valid and red when invalid so players know before placing.
- 2024-04-05 ByteBlox [20:46](https://youtu.be/cWfsm8rwgDg?t=1246): Button press handlers must be LocalScripts inside the GUI, since a press is local to that player; use button.Activated:Connect.
- 2024-04-05 ByteBlox [18:43](https://youtu.be/cWfsm8rwgDg?t=1123): Use Scale instead of Offset for UI sizes so elements scale across phone, tablet and PC screen sizes.
- 2024-04-05 ByteBlox [21:57](https://youtu.be/cWfsm8rwgDg?t=1317): Changes to StarterGui at runtime don't affect players already spawned; modify the player's PlayerGui instead.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/viewportframe-masking/2964839): For image masks on a 0-alpha mesh, set SurfaceAppearance AlphaMode to Transparency; it supports only 2 or 5 transparency levels, which causes banding.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Use built-in Roblox UI tools for simple static UIs; adopt React (react-lua) when UI complexity grows and needs structure.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Always pass an explicit dependency array to useEffect; omitting it reruns the effect every render and can cause infinite loops.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Clean up connections (e.g. RunService.Heartbeat, UserInputService signals) by returning a cleanup function from useEffect that calls Disconnect.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Style Roblox GUI by adding child objects like UIListLayout, UICorner, UIPadding and UIStroke inside the element's children table.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Treat useRef access to underlying GUIObjects as a last resort; it breaks unidirectional data flow and makes code harder to reason about.
- 2024-06-02 AlvinBlox [5:28](https://youtu.be/paRHxtuk_Mc?t=328): On player join, set the status label to the current value of the shared StringValue so new players don't see stale or blank text before the next change.
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [0:00](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=0): Build progress bars as an outer frame plus an inner fill frame sized 1,1 in the parent; change only the inner frame's X scale.
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [2:39](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=159): Compute progress as current/target (e.g. cash/achievement) so it returns a 0-1 decimal, then pass it to UDim2.fromScale(n,1).
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [1:24](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=84): UICorner on very small inner fill frames creates visual artifacts; the creator avoids rounded corners on progress bars for this reason.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [0:00](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=0): Set BillboardGui size to 4,0.20 with the frame at scale 1,1 so the overhead UI fills a clean rectangle.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [0:41](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=41): Use StudsOffsetWorldSpace (e.g. 0,3,0) to float overhead GUI above the head rather than on it.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [1:23](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=83): Set TextLabel TextScaled on and frame/label background transparency to 1 for a clean floating tag look.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [5:57](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=357): Enable AlwaysOnTop on the BillboardGui so it is not hidden behind other parts, and tune LightInfluence for brightness.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [9:45](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=585): Bind a text label to an Attribute with GetAttributeChangedSignal so the displayed cash updates automatically when the value changes.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [12:35](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=755): Update the cost and owned-amount text labels whenever a new item is created or purchased, otherwise the UI shows stale values.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [14:00](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=840): Use LayoutOrder instead of renaming frames to control display order in a UIGridLayout or UIListLayout.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [15:02](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=902): Style buy buttons as bold, bright, clickable elements and make stat labels gray so players can tell buttons from readouts.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [5:53](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=353): Use a text-effects module to add six animated text effects to shop signs so the shop feels alive.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [1:25](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=85): Set UI sizes in Scale (0.1–1) rather than Offset so elements resize with the screen; Roblox doesn't stretch them by default.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [4:12](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=252): Store a colour palette in a Configuration (Color3 values) with a fixed role per colour: base, outline, button outline, text. Keeps the GUI consistent.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [6:13](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=373): Learn the UI modifiers (UIStroke, UICorner, UIGradient) and use gradients on key text like headers to add polish.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [6:45](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=405): Make custom button and icon images in a design tool rather than basic frames; custom art makes a UI stand out from most games using defaults.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [10:05](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=605): Animate panels open and closed with TweenService (about 0.1–0.2s, Back easing) instead of making them blink in and out.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [14:51](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=891): Add MouseEnter hover tweens on buttons to make the UI feel responsive.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [16:06](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=966): Keep menus uncluttered and easy for a new player to understand; overly complex UI drives players away.
- 2024-08-22 PineappleDev [2:14](https://youtu.be/5wudgfIoTwo?t=134): Show queue status with a BillboardGui above the queue area: player count out of limit and a countdown timer text.
- 2024-09-08 BubuTheDev [2:48](https://youtu.be/zc7vWpLOejE?t=168): Place the FPS label in a screen corner (right-aligned, slightly down) so it does not block gameplay HUD.
- 2024-09-19 GnomeCode [14:05](https://youtu.be/oEWoyT-GBZY?t=845): Uncheck ResetOnSpawn on the ScreenGui so the status label persists across respawns.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [27:28](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=1648): Add a progress bar to goal-based games like obbies or tycoons; scale its bar width by cleared/total count, computed from #folder:GetChildren() so it updates automatically.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [29:32](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=1772): Set ScreenGui ResetOnSpawn to false so progress UI persists across respawns.
- 2024-10-12 GnomeCode [10:30](https://youtu.be/H9CbgCe47Mo?t=630): Disable the spawn location's force field; the default glittering shield breaks immersion, especially in first-person.
- 2024-10-12 SimTek Game Development [19:06](https://youtu.be/i2_7zT36Jeg?t=1146): Show a billboard GUI above PVP-tagged characters so other players can see who is flagged; clone it server-side on tag change and remove it on switch back.
- 2024-10-12 SimTek Game Development [27:16](https://youtu.be/i2_7zT36Jeg?t=1636): Set the toggle button's AnchorPoint so it stays anchored to its corner when the UI is resized across screen sizes.
- 2024-10-12 SimTek Game Development [10:46](https://youtu.be/i2_7zT36Jeg?t=646): Use Activated rather than Activate on UI buttons so the toggle works on mobile as well as PC.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [8:53](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=533): Use UIGradient transparency to make ViewportFrame content fade based on view angle, creating parts visible only when looked at.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [14:05](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=845): Duplicate the ViewportFrame and rotate copies (90, 180, 270 degrees) to create multi-angle clone effects.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [16:06](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=966): Animate UIGradient Rotation with TweenService (3s, linear, repeat infinite, reverses) for moving visual effects.
- 2024-11-03 ByteBlox [18:51](https://youtu.be/4yIjd9Xn9sE?t=1131): Give visual feedback on currency progress, e.g. lerp character color from gray to green as balance approaches a target (clamp percentage to 1).
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [5:11](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=311): Compact the shop by merging similar categories (boosts and potions) and make game pass inventory button larger, since players were confused where to find passes.
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [8:45](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=525): Add multi-select for inventory actions like lock and delete (click-to-select border, hover-to-select) to replace drag selection that was removed for lag.
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [11:04](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=664): Give potions their own inventory frame showing counts and use buttons; players had requested it.
- 2024-11-16 PineappleDev [0:10](https://youtu.be/7ufBtbIOdZU?t=10): Build the flyout as a hidden template GUI and clone it per reward rather than creating UI from scratch each time.
- 2024-11-16 PineappleDev [2:14](https://youtu.be/7ufBtbIOdZU?t=134): Tween the HUD counter's size slightly bigger then reverse it to create a pop effect when currency arrives.
- 2024-11-16 PineappleDev [4:16](https://youtu.be/7ufBtbIOdZU?t=256): Convert random pixel offsets to scale (divide by 100) so flyouts spread correctly across different window sizes.
- 2024-11-16 PineappleDev [4:16](https://youtu.be/7ufBtbIOdZU?t=256): Reset the target's position before each flyout so window resizing doesn't break tween destinations.
- 2024-11-16 PineappleDev [5:37](https://youtu.be/7ufBtbIOdZU?t=337): Shrink the clone to size zero when sending it to the HUD so it disappears unobtrusively as it arrives.
- 2024-11-17 ByteBlox [8:05](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=485): Set UI sizes to Scale (not Offset) and set Scale to 0 for Offset so elements resize proportionally across screen sizes.
- 2024-11-17 ByteBlox [15:18](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=918): Use Activated instead of MouseButton1Click for buttons so taps on touchscreens and controller presses also register.
- 2024-11-17 ByteBlox [24:10](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=1450): Offer players a choice to switch between PC and a lower-quality mobile graphics mode.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [11:37](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=697): Fix glitched icon images before release; a missing darkened variant made the reward icon display incorrectly.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [14:30](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=870): Make each egg icon show its pet so players recognise what is inside before opening.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [19:08](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=1148): Billboard notifications can obstruct the view; remove them if they clutter the area.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [16:35](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=995): Open up spawn areas: moving areas aside and expanding the lobby made the hub feel more open and better looking.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [21:12](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=1272): Randomise the loading screen from a set of images so returning players see something different each time.
- 2025-01-04 PineappleDev [3:49](https://youtu.be/E2nFsr22A1M?t=229): Restyle the queue UI freely, but keep the Players and Time text object names unchanged or the system will break.
- 2025-01-05 ByteBlox [15:01](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=901): Disable PlayerGui ScreenGuis and set player.CameraMode to LockFirstPerson for a more immersive horror view; CameraMode animates the transition.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [4:55](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=295): Icons taken from screenshots with backgrounds removed are hard to read on dark backgrounds; design custom icons per item and test contrast.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [0:47](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=47): Replace generic simulator-style sell pads with a diegetic NPC merchant that has dialogue, which makes selling feel less generic.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [10:29](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=629): Hide dialogue UI by default and toggle Enabled from the dialogue function; calling it with nil hides the box.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [13:15](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=795): Make dialogue click-to-skip only after typing finishes, so players must read long lines instead of spamming through them.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [3:33](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=213): Use a UIAspectRatioConstraint on character images so they scale without stretching; the speaker used 1.29.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [4:14](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=254): Set the character image ZIndex above the dialogue frame so the portrait sits in front of the text box.
- 2025-02-14 Duckable [4:10](https://youtu.be/6g8ZYtDKPBw?t=250): Gather screenshots of other tycoons and make a mood board (Figma) before designing the building menu in Studio.
- 2025-02-14 Duckable [5:32](https://youtu.be/6g8ZYtDKPBw?t=332): Show a buy/sell cost popup so players see exactly how much they spent or refunded; use world-space pop-ups over billboard GUIs for text.
- 2025-02-18 GnomeCode [10:28](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=628): Don't rely on a one-time UserInputService.TouchEnabled check; hook LastInputTypeChanged to show/hide the mobile crouch button dynamically as the input type changes.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [7:39](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=459): Use a clear teleport/transition screen so players understand the delay between activating a teleport and loading.
- 2025-03-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/text-custom-fonts-advanced-control/3521684): Use Text+ scaling instead of TextScaled when you need text to fit identically across devices; the library handles size differences more precisely.
- 2025-03-03 PineappleDev [0:40](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=40): Put buttons inside a Frame with a UIListLayout so resizing a button on hover doesn't break the layout; buttons can differ in size.
- 2025-03-03 PineappleDev [2:32](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=152): Use TweenService with Back easing for hover grow (overshoot) and a faster Quad/out tween for un-hover to make buttons feel polished.
- 2025-03-03 PineappleDev [4:27](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=267): Store hover and unhover tweens in a table keyed by button name so they can be played from MouseEnter/MouseLeave/click handlers.
- 2025-03-03 PineappleDev [3:54](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=234): Skip click sounds on TextBoxes if you don't want a sound when players click text fields; check with IsA checks.
- 2025-03-18 ArTheDev [14:20](https://youtu.be/V7PdX03D_hM?t=860): Animate XP bar changes with TweenService (about 2 seconds) instead of snapping, so progress feels smooth.
- 2025-03-20 ArTheDev [2:05](https://youtu.be/5QZA1_0L9PU?t=125): Build the party UI with a scrolling list layout and a template frame in ReplicatedStorage; clone one template per member so the list stays sorted.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [2:18](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=138): Outsource cartoon vector icons to a designer when possible; the creator made one in 10 seconds but relies on a designer for the rest.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [7:09](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=429): Customise the hover highlight, item UI text labels and fonts in the item UI templates; return edited UI to ReplicatedStorage afterwards.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [9:13](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=553): Item tags (valuable, fuel, usable, heavy) are conditional on item data; add new tags by copying a tag template and changing its condition and text.
- 2025-04-10 BloxxedDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/RVZBjAUzf7o?t=41): Apply a blur to the background to draw focus to the play area so players aren't distracted or lost.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [2:40](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=160): Build polished UI and a progress bar showing each player's position on the hill so players can read their status at a glance.
- 2025-05-07 JustDucksey [1:23](https://youtu.be/o-8KX6XgC7s?t=83): Use a screenshot of a reference UI as a placeholder background to mock up shop layouts fast.
- 2025-05-07 JustDucksey [2:47](https://youtu.be/o-8KX6XgC7s?t=167): Duplicate one item UI into a scrolling frame, then rename and reprice each entry.
- 2025-05-08 PineappleDev [0:17](https://youtu.be/Iz2ljOdZRJI?t=17): Structure menus as one Frame containing all menu contents so a single visibility toggle opens and closes the whole menu.
- 2025-05-08 PineappleDev [2:16](https://youtu.be/Iz2ljOdZRJI?t=136): Read the default FOV from the camera at runtime and add an offset (e.g. +20) so you can tweak FOV without editing the script.
- 2025-05-08 PineappleDev [2:16](https://youtu.be/Iz2ljOdZRJI?t=136): Store the default blur size in a variable instead of hardcoding zero so existing background blur isn't wiped when menus close.
- 2025-05-21 JustDucksey [5:25](https://youtu.be/qVkYbVHWI1I?t=325): Duplicate an existing shop frame, update its attributes and price, and set the buy button text to match the crop cost.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [3:20](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=200): Add polish cheaply: fading text on currency change and hover/press feedback on buttons make UI feel responsive.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [5:16](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=316): Rebuild a bad UI rather than patching it; Dev 3 found the existing UI poor and restyled it for consistency.
- 2025-05-25 GnomeCode [9:57](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=597): Use a ProximityPrompt with a 1 second hold and show the item name as action text and price as object text.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [9:22](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=562): Make it clear where vehicles/mounts are kept and add a visible pickup prompt; hidden horses cost players minutes.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [13:15](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=795): Auto-reload or single-button reload for consumable weapons; manual multi-step loading is frustrating and easy to forget.
- 2025-06-08 Cumin [13:56](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=836): Show clear HUD warnings for durability or ammo running out, with sound cues, so players aren't surprised mid-fight.
- 2025-06-14 JustDucksey [1:24](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=84): Show a fuel display with the exact number alongside the bar so players can read resource levels precisely.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [11:54](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=714): Check that tutorial arrows don't overlap notifications; the arrow overlapped the notification in testing.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [10:31](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=631): Add a low graphics setting that disables shadows for mobile players and a music volume setting for annoying background tracks.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [11:54](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=714): Use CanvasGroups to stop icons glitching outside frames during UI animations.
- 2025-06-16 ArTheDev [15:51](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=951): Disable the ProximityPrompt on the client after unlocking so the prompt stops showing for already-unlocked locations.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [0:23](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=23): Use custom proximity prompts with better visibility and a highlight when players are close to a target.
- 2025-07-17 Roblox [17:52](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=1072): Show contextual, real-time nudges when chat gets edgy; Roblox reports abuse reports fell by more than half after subtle nudges in voice chat.
- 2025-07-31 ArTheDev [16:16](https://youtu.be/_6F6vRt_9sM?t=976): Use UIListLayout with newest cooldown on top and scale (not offset) sizing so bars shrink correctly inside their container.
- 2025-07-31 ArTheDev [20:23](https://youtu.be/_6F6vRt_9sM?t=1223): Drive cooldown bar updates with RunService Heartbeat and disconnect previous connections when an ability is re-triggered to avoid duplicate updaters.
- 2025-09-01 PineappleDev [3:21](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=201): On the client, hide the buy button once all tiers are owned; otherwise prompt the next product ID indexed by owned count plus one.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [17:14](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1034): Check UserInputService.TouchEnabled to show the on-screen sprint button only on touch devices; keyboard users get Shift.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [18:36](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1116): Early-return on gameProcessed in InputBegan so chat and other UI input does not trigger sprint.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [40:47](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=2447): Drive the stamina bar from the stamina value's Changed event on the client; use scale-based size and color thresholds (green above 66, yellow above 33, red below).
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [45:39](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=2739): Use a UIListLayout set to horizontal to stack a stamina label and bar, and size children with scale so they fit the frame.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [7:25](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=445): Bind controller buttons (e.g. R1) and keypad keys alongside keyboard keys so the movement works across devices.
- 2025-10-09 JustDucksey [1:24](https://youtu.be/XZKHQPObHgw?t=84): Switch from third person to first person when third person is too hard to control for the core interaction.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [11:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=716): Design UI to work on mobile as well as PC from the start, as the backpack system was made to work on mobile.
- 2025-11-04 Possible Panda [2:36](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=156): Build Fusion UI in a client-side script. Rendering on the client uses the player's CPU/GPU and keeps server load down.
- 2025-11-04 Possible Panda [8:16](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=496): Use Scale UDim2 values and AnchorPoint (0.5,0.5) for position and size so layouts scale across screen sizes.
- 2025-11-04 Possible Panda [11:51](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=711): Set TextScaled = true so text fills its container instead of using a fixed text size.
- 2025-11-04 Possible Panda [19:21](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=1161): Wrap sibling components in a UIListLayout inside a parent frame so they stack instead of overlapping.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [2:20](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=140): Create a ModuleScript ending in .story per component; expose controls (color, text size) so you can tweak props live in the preview.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [5:00](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=300): Wrap controlled props in Fusion used-as so they accept either a raw value or a Fusion state, matching the story's control input.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [7:03](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=423): Use Mount in Widget to dock the preview in a separate window on a second monitor and see live changes like corner radius.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [9:42](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=582): Build reusable button variants (color, image, text, toggle, radio) as configurable components so one component covers many states.
- 2025-12-12 Poke [4:06](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=246): Test UI scaling early across screen sizes; the creator lost about 5 hours fighting UI scale, so plan layouts with scale-based sizing.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [28:30](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1710): Show a Coming Soon sign for unfinished features instead of an NPC error. Players who skip patch notes otherwise get confused.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [27:10](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1630): Have NPCs and quest-givers do useful things. Filler NPCs with no reason to exist make the world feel empty.
- 2026-01-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/completing-the-dynamic-head-migration/4301387): Consolidate separate Classic Heads and Classic Faces UI categories into a single unified Heads category.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [8:06](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=486): Use consistent, discoverable access for menus. Mixing glowing circles, keybinds and randomly placed stands with no signage hides the upgrade menu.
- 2026-02-07 Cumin [8:47](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=527): Show crafting materials in the inventory. Players expect to check their resources there and will be confused without them.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [3:08](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=188): Clean up keybinds and small annoyances; the speaker says small changes can have a big impact on feel.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [4:43](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=283): Add billboard UI for enemies (name and health), and scale UI correctly so it does not render oversized.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Shift Lock and Sprint both bind to Left Shift today, causing conflicts; test input defaults carefully and give feedback on cross-platform keybinds.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [13:22](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=802): Build UI from a few parts only: text buttons, text labels and frames, styled with UICorner, UIGradient, UIStroke and Montserrat ExtraBold text.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [3:08](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=188): Buy a UI template pack for about $3-$5 and reuse it across games instead of hiring a UI designer.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [8:29](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=509): Set camera Type to Scriptable while following a part, and reset it to Custom when the target is missing to restore default control.
- 2026-04-06 ArTheDev [22:35](https://youtu.be/RDjsR4iIRlg?t=1355): Build the inventory GUI as a UIGridLayout frame with a cloned item template (name label plus amount label), toggled visible for testing.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Keep custom UI out of roughly 60px from each screen side; default touch movement and jump buttons are moving into those zones.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Check the new touch button placement in Studio Emulator or on a physical device for overlaps with custom UI after the April 30 update.
- 2026-04-12 Stewiepfing [10:15](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=615): Scale ScrollingFrame ScrollBarThickness to about 2% of parent AbsoluteSize.X on touch-only devices so scrollbars look consistent on phones and desktop.
- 2026-04-12 Stewiepfing [13:44](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=824): Set ProximityPrompt Style to Custom and use the Hologram module to get a 3D billboard prompt with beam, always-on-top, and dynamic UI options.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [23:45](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=1425): Debounce click-to-advance reveals (about 0.5s) so players cannot spam through the summon animation.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [27:11](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=1631): Tween a background blur on summon start and reset it on finish to focus attention on the reveal.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [4:22](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=262): Design original UI instead of copying genre-standard styles; he prototyped in Figma before implementing to differentiate from similar games.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [1:27](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=87): Replace cash-grabby UI with a cleaner custom design; the creator remade every frame in Studio after mocking up in Photoshop.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [4:49](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=289): Use ViewportFrames to play animations inside shop item cards; it looks more engaging than static icons.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [5:30](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=330): Add a custom speed selector so high-speed players can slow down and control their character more easily.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Use per-corner UICorner radii (TopLeftRadius etc.) for speech bubbles, tab headers and one-sided callouts instead of image assets or 9-slice workarounds.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Use UIShadow for depth on buttons and glows; it does not affect layout or trigger recalculations, and multiple UIShadows can be stacked on one element.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): For TextLabel/TextBox/TextButton, UIShadow applies to the background rectangle, not the text; native text shadows are not available.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Avoid setting both CornerRadius and individual corner radii when styling a UICorner; it produces unexpected behaviour. Pick one approach.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Setting CornerRadius overwrites all per-corner properties; set CornerRadius first, then override individual corners in Properties.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Shadows may look jagged on elements with large corner radius; a known issue under investigation.
- 2026-05-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263): Use low BlurRadius for hard edges and high values (up to 1000) for soft diffuse glows; scale values are a percentage of the parent's shortest dimension.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [3:56](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=236): Change the game's font globally with a single command to save time on UI styling.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [3:56](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=236): Add clean UI animations; the creator says they make the game feel more alive.
- 2026-06-12 GosuGamers [page](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78614-grow-a-garden-2-has-launched-on-roblox-here-s-everything-new-added-in-this-sequel): Consolidating scattered shop booths into one central hub reduces walking time for day-to-day management.
- 2026-06-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535): Use Sling to replace the default backpack and group inventory into tab categories (Weapons, Potions, Resources) with declarative predicate functions.
- 2026-06-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535): Customize theme properties or switch between built-in default and light themes rather than building a backpack UI from scratch.
- 2026-06-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535): Programmatically set which items sit in the hotbar and track when players rearrange hotbar items.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [7:21](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=441): Announce level-ups visibly and show new abilities on level-up. The reviewer missed level-ups entirely.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [8:01](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=481): Give exact numbers in status effects and buffs, e.g. 'heals X% of max HP', not vague text like 'applies fractured'.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [8:43](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=523): Show status effects gained from encounters explicitly, with their effects, so players aren't left guessing.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [10:03](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=603): Make choices clear in encounters: don't offer options that do nothing, such as upgrading an incompatible class with no effect.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [6:00](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=360): Give UI feedback for every input and use varied hit sounds and attack animations so actions feel responsive.
- 2026-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505): Call MarketplaceService:OpenShop(player) from custom buttons, NPCs or trigger zones to open Shop while keeping your own UI design; requires PromptExternalPurchase capability.
- 2026-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505): To hide the Shop button in the hamburger menu, call StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.ExperienceShop, false) from a client script.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [0:59](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=59): Set ScreenGui ResetOnSpawn to false so the menu is not destroyed and rebuilt on every respawn, which would reset held state.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [2:05](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=125): Use UDim2 with scale for size/position so layouts adapt to phones, tablets and monitors; offset adds fixed pixels on top.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [2:47](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=167): Set AnchorPoint to 0.5,0.5 when centering a frame with position 0.5,0,0.5,0; otherwise the top-left corner lands at center.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [3:40](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=220): Add a UICorner child to frames and buttons (scale 0, offset ~12) to replace dated sharp 90-degree corners with one instance per element.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [4:37](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=277): Enable TextScaled on text labels and buttons so text resizes to its box instead of clipping on small screens.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [5:31](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=331): Connect TextButton.MouseButton1Click for clicks; it fires on both mouse click and phone tap with no extra code.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [6:37](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=397): Use UIListLayout (padding ~8px, horizontal center) instead of hand-placing elements so the column reflows when items are added or removed.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [7:20](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=440): Add UIPadding to keep content off the frame border; roughly 10px top padding gives breathing room.
- 2026-07-08 Chase Higgins [8:17](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=497): Bind text properties to sources (state) so only the affected property updates instead of rebuilding the whole element on change.
- 2026-07-08 Chase Higgins [9:11](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=551): Use bind.effect to run side effects (prints, animations like Ripple) automatically whenever a source changes, instead of wiring them into click handlers.
- 2026-07-29 Raul [4:46](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=286): Start from a free UI template to speed up menus and buttons, then restyle it; the creator found it saved time on shop and backpack UI.
- 2026-07-29 Raul [8:42](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=522): Replace click-to-use tools with proximity prompts, which the creator found more usable than clicking.
- 2026-08-02 janisjanis01 [4:42](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=282): Match UI style to genre: colourful for simulators, minimalistic for FPS games.
- 2026-08-07 LovJak [4:45](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=285): Add a mute-background-music button; long testing sessions make looping music a problem for players too.
- 2026-08-10 Cumin [12:09](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=729): Add a sell-all option and a proper inventory selection menu; per-item selling with error spam is frustrating.
- 2026-08-10 Cumin [12:09](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=729): Add a sprint button; a 20 base walk speed with no sprint makes travel tedious.
- 2026-08-12 RoyalDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=165): Replace a physical map with UI-based navigation: buttons with clear labels (Play, Select Mode, Shop) so players can't get lost and spend effort on the core loop.
- 2026-08-18 RoyalDev [1:24](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=84): Replace free roaming with a fully GUI-driven flow (e.g., big Play button, on-screen commands) so players can't get lost or wonder what to do next.
- 2026-08-18 RoyalDev [2:06](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=126): Add small animations to every button click to make the interface feel polished and responsive.
- 2026-08-29 MonarchDev [3:16](https://youtu.be/_DwOE49GDdo?t=196): Rebuilding UI to look distinct rather than copy-pasting free UI packs gives the game a unique identity.
- 2026-09-00 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Input action manager and Input Action Label (Late 2026/Early 2027) build cross-platform control mappings with automatic on-screen hotkey hints.
- 2026-09-00 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Upgraded UI gradients, UI blur, UI shadows and 2D particles (Late 2026/Early 2027) add richer UI styling without workarounds.
- 2026-09-10 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/): New UI features (blur, text shadows, improved gradients) are available to enhance game look and feel.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): UI blur, text shadows and improved gradients arrive late 2026 with 2D capabilities (orthographic camera, sprite-sheet import).
- 2026-09-12 LovJak [2:24](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=144): Provide a mobile-friendly way to recover lost items, such as touching the thief, since recovering items on mobile is hard.
- 2026-09-13 Cumin [7:30](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=450): Show full ability stats in descriptions: hit count, cooldown, and exact scaling ratio, not vague phrases like 'scales with spell power'.
- 2026-09-13 Cumin [8:11](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=491): Let players upgrade equipped gear without unequipping it first; forced unequips feel lazy and add friction.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [10:17](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=617): Direct launch: players can create game-specific home-screen shortcuts for one-tap entry, reducing join friction on mobile.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4880336): Set StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation to Disabled and republish if you need more time before Phase 2 enables it by default on September 24.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4880336): Check rotated GuiObjects and Path2D instances for clipping now, since they will be clipped by parent GuiObjects once the property is enabled by default.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-14%E2%80%9320-2026/4880336): Test rotated GUI elements and Path2D before September 24, when StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation becomes default; temporarily set it to Disabled and republish if needed.
- 2026-09-20 Possible Panda [9:02](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=542): Use a computed to derive UI values from atoms; Charm's computed tracks dependencies automatically, similar to Fusion's computed.
- 2026-09-20 Possible Panda [10:24](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=624): Bridge Charm atoms into Fusion with a small helper, letting UI bind to game state that lives outside components.
- 2026-09-27 janisjanis01 [7:10](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=430): Test mobile input early; buttons that cannot be clicked on mobile cause immediate exits.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Investor Relations [page](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm): 2D tools now include orthographic camera, animated image containers, blur and text shadows for 2D game creation.
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [5:12](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=312): Replace placeholder emojis with real art early so menus match the game style instead of being rebuilt later.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=164): Attach reference screenshots to each UI item in the design doc so developers know the target look.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [1:31](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=91): Replace default Roblox gray buttons with a custom styled HUD; default UI reads as amateur. Optimize touch controls for mobile.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [2:08](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=128): Replace flat grey default buttons with custom semi-transparent frosted-glass UI and optimise it for mobile touch.
- 2026-10-12 Xaiyne [5:45](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=345): Use a thumbnail maker plugin to preview how the accessory looks in store listings before uploading.
- 2026-10-16 MonarchDev [4:44](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=284): Point the AI to the exact UI side you want and ask it to check its own work; UI packs help.
- 2026-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-12-18-2026/4938638): Upgraded UIShadow (beta, File > Beta Features) adds text, knockout, inset shadows and gradient fills; live experiences cannot yet publish with these.
- 2026-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-12-18-2026/4938638): Device Simulator is out of beta: switch devices with mouse wheel, see resolutions in dropdown, toggle Network Simulation more easily.
- 2026-10-24 Crusherfire [6:24](https://youtu.be/4n7thZAAF-E?t=384): Expect AI-built UI to need several hours of back-and-forth refinement: fix consistency, missing elements, and replace animations you dislike before shipping.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Set ImageLabel.ResampleMode to Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated for crisp pixel art scaling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/weapons-kit): Provide SmallTouchscreen and LargeTouchscreen AimButton/FireButton frames so the prefab weapons work on touch devices.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/weapons-kit): Hit feedback: the HitMarker and directional indicators (e.g. red arc showing shot direction) help players read combat; damage billboards show only to the shooter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset): Send the end-state message to the status GUI before any wait; put task.wait(transitionTime) after updateStatus or players never see the message.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Combine UI objects with scripts for interactive HUD elements such as a custom health meter, tied to in-game state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Use proximity prompts that only appear as players approach 3D objects, keeping the interface uncluttered.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Custom chat UIs can no longer render messages; place a TextChannelWindow in a ScreenGui and bind its Target to a TextChannel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat): Set ChatWindowConfiguration.TextChannelDisplayMode to DefaultTextChannels in Studio so a custom channel shows only in its own window; scripts cannot change it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/connect-the-beam): Enable FaceCamera on beams so they stay visible from any camera angle.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/connect-the-beam): Use contrasting or saturated colors for guide beams so they draw attention and show players where to look.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/connect-the-beam): Choose beam colors that remain distinguishable for colour-blind players, so the guide reaches the broadest audience.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/creating-a-gui): Test the GUI both with and without the minimum player count to confirm each status message displays correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Use FeaturePackagesCore.ModalManager.getHudGui() to fetch the shared HUD ScreenGui, which holds two frames for HUD button placement.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Reduced hover animation duration from 0.5 to 0.3 seconds for a snappier feel; UIHover now adjusts TextTransparency/BackgroundTransparency on text buttons instead of resizing them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Use a consistent HUD button style across all packages (padding, corner radius, UIHover tag) so the UI looks cohesive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/gamepad): Map secondary actions (reload, targeting, inventory, minimap) to ButtonL1, ButtonR1, ButtonX, or ButtonY for consistency with other games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/2D-paths): Path2D must sit under a ScreenGui or SurfaceGui; it does not need to be a direct child of that container.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/2D-paths): Path2D control point positions are relative to the parent container, so nesting the path in a Frame changes its effective position.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/2D-paths): Use GetPositionOnCurveArcLength(t) rather than raw parameter t to get evenly spaced positions along the curve.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/2D-paths): Tween a NumberValue from 0 to 1 and drive GuiObject.Position from its Changed event to animate UI along a path.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/2D-paths): Breaking tangents lets each side move independently; Mirror Tangents will not visibly update the path until a tangent marker is dragged again.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/device-simulator): Check layouts across phone, tablet and console to surface safe area, overflow and anchoring problems immediately.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/device-simulator): Verify tap targets and text in Physical size mode after calibrating, comparing a phone against a tablet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/device-simulator): Focus a text field on a simulated phone and confirm the on-screen keyboard does not cover controls the player needs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/device-simulator): Calibrate physical scaling once by matching the on-screen width to a student ID or library card; the value persists across sessions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Build diegetic UI inside the 3D world (SurfaceGui on parts) rather than screen overlays to keep players immersed in the world.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Mock UI in an external app first, then export pieces as .png and import into Studio to iterate on look quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Non-interactive SurfaceGuis can sit directly on a part; interactive ones must live in StarterGui with Adornee set to the target part so button events work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Fake parallax by stacking three ScreenGuis on three parts at different X values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Set SurfaceGui.LightInfluence below 1 to enable Brightness, then raise Brightness to create a glow effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Test UI with Device Emulator set to Actual Resolution across Phone, Tablet, Desktop and Console so text and icons stay legible and don't crowd the 3D view.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Use UIAspectRatioConstraint on key UI frames and icons so their proportions hold across different screen sizes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Set crosshair ScaleType to Fit and center it at 0.5,0.5 so the image doesn't stretch on various screen sizes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Group UI into multiple ScreenGuis (HUD, selection, overlays) and keep them disabled until context requires them, rather than one always-on screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Place the objective and team score at the top-middle of the screen, and the crosshair/hit feedback at center, to match importance and focal point.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Use TextScaled on labels and a consistent font such as Montserrat Medium/Bold to keep text readable at every screen size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/landing): Show the current game state and match info to players through a GUI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/get-off-the-ground): If Explorer and Properties panels are missing, toggle them on from Studio's Window menu. Keep both open for building and scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain): Set Editor Quality Level to the highest in Studio Settings to preview all water properties while editing; some are only visible in playtest.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts): Tune MaxActivationDistance per object (example: 4 studs for a lock) to push interaction closer to the target instead of relying on the default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Use UIGridLayout for equal-sized tiles such as shop inventories; use UITableLayout when items need to sort into related rows and columns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Once UIGridLayout or UITableLayout is inserted, setting Position or Size on sibling objects in properties or scripts will not have normal effect; the layout controls them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Use FillDirectionMaxCells on UIGridLayout to cap cells per row or column for finer control.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Set UIGridStyleLayout.SortOrder to Enum.SortOrder.Name to order grid items alphabetically; default is LayoutOrder.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Add UISizeConstraint MinSize larger than CellSize to let a grid object span multiple cells.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): In UITableLayout, a header cell's UISizeConstraint MinSize sets the width or height of the entire column or row it belongs to.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts): Enable UITableLayout FillEmptySpaceColumns or FillEmptySpaceRows if cell sizes should not be dictated by child GuiObject sizes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/create-flickering-lights): Use the NumberSequence editor's envelope lines on a keypoint to randomise brightness between a min and max for less repetitive flicker.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script): Set the experience to Public in Experience Settings > Permissions so friends can join via link; new publishes default to private.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Don't model detail players can't see; a distant image on a decal can convey depth cheaply.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/selfie-mode): When using a custom open button, call setHudButtonEnabled(false) first to remove the default camera button, then connect your button to openSelfieMode().
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/selfie-mode): Customise look via setTheme (textSize, font, backgroundColor, button and tooltip colours, padding) to match your game's visual style.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): On mobile, ActionSlot 1-7 map to on-screen touch buttons; set CustomIcon/CustomIconActive/CustomIconInvalid asset IDs for those buttons.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Use UIScale to zoom in while designing detailed UI in Studio; it scales children and UIStroke/UICorner proportionally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Tween UIScale to animate a button, e.g. slightly enlarge on mouse hover.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Use AutomaticSize so labels expand for long localized text across many languages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Set a GuiObject's Size to 0 along an axis with AutomaticSize if it should shrink to fit children; a nonzero Size sets a minimum.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Use TextWrapped with AutomaticSize Y or XY so text labels grow vertically when wrapping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Use ScrollingFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize instead of AutomaticSize for scrolling containers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Keep UITextSizeConstraint MinTextSize at 9 or higher; lower values are hard for many viewers to read.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Use UIAspectRatioConstraint with AspectRatio 1 on square ImageLabels such as avatar thumbnails to prevent stretching.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): A UISizeConstraint or UIAspectRatioConstraint overrides UIListLayout sizing on the same object; avoid combining them unintentionally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers): Constraints must be direct children of the GuiObject they constrain.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/proximity-prompts): Set ObjectText and ActionText so players know what the object is and what pressing the key will do.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/proximity-prompts): Use Exclusivity OneGlobally when several prompts sit in one area to reduce prompt clutter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/proximity-prompts): Avoid AlwaysShow unless needed: all visible prompts with the same keycode fire on one keypress.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Use UIAspectRatioConstraint plus UISizeConstraint (e.g. MaxSize inf, 44) so buttons scale with screen height but cap pixel size on phones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Enable the Device Emulator (phone such as iPhone X or Galaxy A51, Fit to Window) to verify UI fits small screens before publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Set LayoutOrder on siblings and keep layout targets as siblings of UIListLayout, not children, or the layout will not position them correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Give a settings menu a single parent Frame so it can be managed as one stateful object that toggles open or closed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Pair every toggle button with an explicit close button inside the menu so players have two ways to dismiss it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Use a 0-duration TweenInfo for instant state changes such as hiding a menu, and cancel running tweens before starting a new state to avoid conflicts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Reuse tweens by creating them once per state in a hydrate function rather than creating new tweens on every state change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Use UIDragDetector with TranslateLine and BoundingUI linked to the track frame to build draggable sliders; sync the handle position and fill size in one setValue function.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mobile): Set StarterGui.ScreenOrientation (default LandscapeSensor) in Studio properties; lock to Portrait only when the experience needs it, e.g. a minigame.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-styles): Use error alerts very sparingly; reserve them for critical warnings only.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-styles): Use warning alerts as a heads-up, info for teacher-related comments, and success for non-crucial extra information.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-styles): Limit learning objectives to a maximum of 4 per module, ending each with a period and bolding the key concept.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/buttons): Use SecondaryActivated for right-click on desktop and long press on mobile so secondary actions work cross-platform.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/buttons): Connect button logic to Activated, which fires on click or tap, rather than raw input events for cross-platform support.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/buttons): Use ImageButton HoverImage and PressedImage variants to give visual feedback on hover and press.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players): Disable default inventory GUI with StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled in a LocalScript before adding your own inventory UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players): Set StarterGui.ResetPlayerGuiOnSpawn deliberately; when true, PlayerGui is cleared and replaced from StarterGui on every respawn.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/get-started): Close unneeded Studio windows and keep only Explorer and Properties open to reduce clutter while coding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/plugins): Set ClickableWhenViewportHidden = true on toolbar buttons so the plugin can be used when the 3D viewport is hidden.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/rich-text): Set RichText = true per object (TextLabel, TextButton, TextBox); it is off by default and must be enabled individually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/rich-text): Toggle the RichText checkbox in Studio's Properties window to preview the final render and catch broken tags while editing Text.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/rich-text): Escape special characters (&gt; &quot; &apos; &amp;) so they are not parsed as rich text tags, e.g. write 100 &gt; 10.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/rich-text): Close nested tags in reverse order of opening, e.g. <b><i>text</i></b>.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/rich-text): Font weight accepts names (Thin, ExtraLight, Light, Regular, Medium, SemiBold, Bold, ExtraBold, Heavy) or numbers 100-900 in steps of 100; font face names come from the Font enum.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-indoor-environments): Use ExposureCompensation rather than Brightness to lift the scene; Brightness washes out shadows into murkiness while exposure brightens highlights more.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/profile-card): Call ProfileCard.configure() from a LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts to override default styling and behaviour, rather than editing the module.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/profile-card): Set maxClickDistance to limit how far away clicking a character opens its card; default is 100 studs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/profile-card): Keep isTruncated true (default) so overly long strings are hidden rather than overflowing the card.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects): For speech-to-text, set VoiceChatService.UseAudioApi to Enabled and set AudioDeviceInput.Player to the local player at runtime.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/get-started): Close Studio windows you don't need and keep Explorer and Properties open on the right to free space for coding or building.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark): Use a literal in-world UI layer, such as parallax map effects and in-world holo-screens, to add atmosphere.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/bubble-chat): Use BubbleChatConfiguration Properties window for basic changes (text color, spacing); use a LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts for runtime overrides that take precedence over Studio settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/bubble-chat): Set ScaleType to Slice and configure SliceCenter/SliceScale when using 9-sliced bubble background images so corners stay undistorted.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/bubble-chat): Keep MaxDistance and MinimizeDistance in mind: bubbles beyond MaxDistance (default 100 studs) hide, and those beyond MinimizeDistance (default 40) collapse into an ellipsis bubble.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mouse-and-keyboard): Support mouse/keyboard: a large percentage of Roblox sessions are on PC/laptop, so design inputs for it from the start.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mouse-and-keyboard): Customize the mouse icon via UserInputService.MouseIcon to match UI style, and swap it temporarily (e.g. when hovering buttons).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mouse-and-keyboard): Lock the mouse with MouseBehavior LockCurrentPosition or LockCenter for camera control; reset to Enum.MouseBehavior.Default to unlock.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Avoid tweening UIStroke.Thickness on text objects; it renders and stores many glyph sizes each frame, causing lag or text flicker.
