- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Use UICorner Scale 0.5 or higher to get a pill shape regardless of width or height; use Offset for fixed pixel radii.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Do not give sibling UIStroke or UIShadow instances the same ZIndex when their render order matters; order is undefined.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Use UIStroke Transparency separate from text transparency to make hollow outlined text or borders.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Set UIShadow.ShowBehindParent to false on transparent parents so the shadow does not darken content behind them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Use UIGradient TileMode Mirror for seamless repetition; Repeat can show a seam if first and last values differ.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/appearance-modifiers): Gradient and upgraded UIShadow features are beta; enable via File > Beta Features before relying on them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Detect the primary input with UserInputService.PreferredInput, not TouchEnabled; TouchEnabled is true on touch-screen laptops, so it is not a reliable mobile check.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Connect to UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal('PreferredInput') to swap UI layouts when the player switches devices mid-session.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Avoid GetLastInputType for layout decisions; it flickers between MouseMovement and Keyboard, and between Touch and Gamepad, causing UI to flicker. Use it only for advanced control schemes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Override Roblox default bindings only in specific cases; players already know the default movement, camera and interaction controls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Do not try to rebind reserved inputs (Esc menu, F9 console, F11, F12, PrintScreen); they always keep their built-in purpose.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input): Disable the backpack, player list or chat features before reusing their keys (Tab, backtick, /), since those actions are reserved until the feature is disabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera): Choose a DevCameraOcclusionMode: Zoom pulls the camera in when the character is hidden; Invisicam fades the occluding object instead, keeping the camera still.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera): Pick movement modes per platform: DevComputerCameraMovementMode for PC and DevTouchCameraMovementMode for phone/tablet, since they are set independently.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera): Use CameraType = Scriptable to fully override the default camera for over-the-shoulder, isometric, or scoped views instead of building a custom follow camera.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/quick-words): Keep each Quick Word under 25 characters so it fits the chat UI; use 3-10 words per category.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge): Use a SurfaceGui with Size {1,0},{1,0} and TextScaled true so the countdown text fills the display part and stays readable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/landing): Use a ClickDetector button to let players buy upgrades that increase carry capacity.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/spawn-respawn): If letting players switch first/third person, change CameraMode programmatically and adjust controls and UI for the perspective change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant): Adjust UI layout with drag-and-drop in Studio rather than describing pixel adjustments in prompts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals): Enable Show Orientation Indicator in Studio right-click menu to confirm which face is Top/Front before assigning Face.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/social-interactions): Chat animations are enabled by default once the module is inserted; set useChatAnimations = false in configure() if you don't want them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui): Use Position, Size, AnchorPoint and ZIndex to control placement and layering of GuiObjects rather than hardcoding screen coordinates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui): Use UIListLayout or grid layouts for repeated item sequences instead of manually positioning each element.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui): Use tweening to transition GuiObjects between states for smooth animation and dynamic visual feedback.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui): Apply universal styling (CSS-like overrides) to share property overrides across many UI instances instead of setting each one manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Use scale values (not offset) for size and position of SurfaceGui child objects so they fit the face regardless of its size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Place interactive SurfaceGui and BillboardGui containers inside StarterGui; buttons only receive input when parented to PlayerGui.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Set the part's CanQuery to true, or interactive UI elements on a SurfaceGui will not receive input.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Add UIAspectRatioConstraint to SurfaceGui children to keep their aspect ratio across face sizes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Enable TextScaled on TextLabels inside a sized BillboardGui so text scales with camera distance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Set Adornee from a script at runtime to attach a BillboardGui to player characters or other dynamic parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Use ZOffset to order multiple SurfaceGuis on the same face; ZOffset does not visually move the container off the surface.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Brightness has no effect when LightInfluence is 1 or AlwaysOnTop is true; lower LightInfluence and raise Brightness (e.g. 10) for dark rooms.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Keep ScreenGuis with interactive elements on CoreUISafeInsets (the default); it keeps UI clear of top bar buttons and cutouts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Use ScreenInsets None only for non-interactive background content, since notches and cutouts can hide it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Use DisplayOrder to layer multiple ScreenGuis, giving modal menus a higher value than the underlying interface.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Toggle ScreenGui.Enabled rather than destroying screens; disabled screens stop rendering and processing input.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Set ResetOnSpawn to false on ScreenGuis placed directly in StarterGui so they persist across respawns and keep state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers): Disable default CoreGui elements (Health, Backpack, PlayerList) via StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled when replacing them with custom UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/micro-gamepad): For TV UI, show a visible focus state, make every control reachable with the four directions, and use large controls with clear spacing for 10-foot viewing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/micro-gamepad): Use center for primary action and back consistently for close/cancel; preserve focus when opening and closing menus.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/labels): Set BackgroundTransparency to 1 on an ImageLabel to show only the image and hide its rectangle.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Set DisplayDistanceType to Subject when you need per-character control; Viewer is the lowest priority and ignored for humanoids set to Subject or None.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Use DisplayWhenDamaged health display to show health bars only on damaged characters, reducing HUD clutter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Team-colored names appear automatically in the team's TeamColor, which helps distinguish teammates from opponents.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/name-health-display): Use EnemyOcclusion so names are hidden only behind walls from opposing team members, keeping teammates' names visible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Use the tabDisplayName, tabOrder and description fields to customize reward tab labels and ordering without code changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Modify Core objects with care, since changes to FeaturePackagesCore.Objects affect all feature packages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Set effect = Types.RewardEffect.Valuable to give high-value rewards a distinct background frame in the UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/emotes): Equipped emotes appear in order set, starting at the top of the wheel and continuing clockwise; key names display as written.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/pickup-system): Pickup prompts show the weapon name and description on proximity; rarity name plus Item appears as the description, so set rarity names clearly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/3D-drag-detectors): Build 3D UI controls like sliders and dimmers by reading DragFrame position via GetPropertyChangedSignal and mapping normalized values to lights or particle properties.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/3D-drag-detectors): Use TranslatePlaneOrLine or TranslateLineOrPlane with the modifier key so players get both precise and free-form drag on PC and gamepad; BestForDevice picks sensible defaults per input.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Add a UIDragDetector as a direct child of any GuiObject to make it draggable across all input types with no code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): UIDragDetectors don't work in Studio while Select, Move, Scale or Rotate tools are active; switch to another tool to test drag.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Use ResponseStyle CustomOffset or CustomScale when you want drag events without the UI moving, then apply your own logic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Set BoundingUI to a container so drag stays inside it; with Scale-based TranslateLine this limits a slider to the container width without extra scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Use SelectionModeDragSpeed with UIDragSpeedAxisMapping to tune X/Y drag speed; XX or YY mapping makes Scale speed use one axis for both.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Set BoundingBehavior to HitPoint when the UI is larger than its bounds, so the grab point stays inside rather than the whole object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-context-menu): Customize ACM look via SetCore("AvatarContextMenuTheme", table) using colors, transparency, Font, and TextScale to match your game's UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/supporting-systems): Show lore as a full-screen ScreenGui dismissed by click, and ambient dialogue as a BillboardGui above the object so it doesn't block the screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/supporting-systems): Give the on-screen centre crosshair context icons (hand, eye, locked door) so players know which objects are interactable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/in-game-sounds): Use RollOffMode InverseTapered for a smoother fade-in as players approach a sound source.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/controller-emulator): Confirm your UI is fully navigable on a TV remote using directional pad, center button and back button.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Restyle package UI by editing colors, fonts and transparency, but do not move objects in the hierarchy without updating the code that finds them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Use the Season Passes Configs/Constants file to adjust sound asset IDs and purchase effect duration and particle colors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Use tag selectors (.ButtonPrimary via CollectionService tags) for reusable styles instead of relying on instance names, since names are not expected to be unique.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Use built-in style queries (@ViewportDisplaySizeSmall/Medium/Large, @PreferredInputTouch, @ReducedMotionEnabledTrue) to adapt UI to screen size, input device and accessibility settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Define shared design values as StyleSheet attributes and reference them with a $ prefix (e.g. $ButtonBgColor) to keep colours and sizes centralised as tokens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Use SetPropertyTransitions with TweenInfo on a StyleRule to animate property changes like colour or rotation on state changes, instead of hand-scripting tweens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Nest StyleRules as children of parent rules to merge selectors and reduce repetition, similar to SCSS nesting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons): Set AutoButtonColor to false when defining custom hover styles so the default button hover colour does not conflict with your rule.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/highlighting): Use highlights to signal that an object is important or interactable, or to show teammates' positions and status.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/highlighting): Use DepthMode AlwaysOnTop to keep important objects visible through walls; Occluded hides them behind obstructions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/tools): Disable RequiresHandle for non-physical tools that only occupy inventory and respond to input.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/emote-bar): Use maxEmotesPerPage to limit visible emotes; smaller screens automatically show fewer. Default is 4. Switch guiType to EmoteWheel for a ring layout.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/emote-bar): Set defaultTempo greater than 0 per emote; tempo is the playback speed factor, and tapping the button rhythmically within tempoActivationWindow (default 3s) changes speed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-4): Customize SurfaceGui and TextLabel properties such as color and font to style in-world signs like the Upgrade Sign.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/use-case-tutorial-overview): Place UI in key on-screen and in-world locations to communicate objectives and location, since players need this to succeed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/greybox-your-environment): Apply distinct placeholder colors to key walls and floors so players can deduce their location and orientation from any visible color.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera/free-camera): Use the Screen GUIs and Leaderboard toggles in the config menu to keep key information visible or hide UI for an unobstructed view.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards): Use IsPrimary BoolValue (true) to put a stat first; if several are primary, Priority decides order. Non-prioritised stats default to priority 0.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards): Hide the PlayerList with StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, false) from a client script during menus or cutscenes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Avoid placing important info or virtual buttons in the bottom-left and bottom-right corners, where default mobile controls sit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Keep frequently used buttons within easy thumb reach; comfortable reach differs between phones and tablets, so test both.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Position custom buttons relative to the default jump button rather than using fixed screen coordinates, for reliable placement across devices.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Show only vital info during active gameplay on mobile; use ProximityPrompts for context actions like opening doors instead of persistent buttons.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Use Scale for responsive positioning and Offset for fixed pixel values; mix both in UDim2 as needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Set AnchorPoint to (0.5, 0.5) when you want position and size changes to scale outward from the object's center.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size): Use layout objects (UIListLayout, UIGridLayout, UITableLayout, UIPageLayout) to organize GuiObjects; they override manual position and size of children.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/photo-booth): Mark your own ScreenGuis with attribute ShowInPhotoBooth=true and use hideOtherGuis/showOtherGuis callbacks to keep HUD elements visible or hide them during photos.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/photo-booth): Keep the default frameMessage fade at 3 seconds or set fadeUiDelay negative to keep the share prompt visible; customise the frame message to fit your game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Use Scale UDim2 values instead of pixel offsets for tween targets so UI lands in the same relative place across screen sizes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Attach a UIAspectRatioConstraint when tweening Size so the element keeps its designed aspect ratio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): For whole-object fades, place the elements in a CanvasGroup and tween GroupTransparency or GroupColor3 instead of tweening each child.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Prefer a UIStroke child over TextStrokeTransparency/TextStrokeColor3 or BorderColor3 for stroke tweens; the docs call it the modern alternative.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Chain tween sequences by calling the next tween's Play inside the previous tween's Completed event.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Default easing is Quad with Out direction; choose Back, Bounce or Elastic for overshoot effects and Linear for constant speed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Set TextLabel.AutoLocalize to false and strip RichText tags before a per-character typewriter reveal, since tags break the character loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Use MaxVisibleGraphemes with utf8.graphemes for typewriter text so multi-byte characters and emoji reveal correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools): Set the tool TextureId to an image of the item for the hotbar icon, and set ToolTip to a short name or description.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/custom-abilities): On mobile, action slots 1-7 map to on-screen buttons; pick slots accordingly when binding abilities for touch players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines): Label all preset UI as 'system preset' in chat and make it visually distinct from native freeform chat.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines): Omit terminal punctuation (! ? .) from presets so they are not confused with general chat messages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design): Plan an aesthetic direction for UI before building; keep art style consistent across all UI elements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design): Wireframe layouts first to design the structure and flow of information before implementing in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design): Use UI for essential gameplay info or to teach unique user flows, not decoration alone.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/speech-to-text): Use push to chat alongside STT for more reliable voice detection, since mics are only active while the player holds a button.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Reduce text and add visuals; replace text with icons and common symbols. Avoid gamer jargon like WASD, use arrow keys wording.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Use familiar UI patterns from similar educational products educators already use, since teachers have little time to plan.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Design for 8-12 year olds with large, minimal GUI and one-hand interactions; 12-15 can use standard commercial game GUI conventions and multiple tasks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings): Meet accessibility needs such as colour contrast, since many schools require accessibility features.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Do not delete or modify the generated BaseStyleSheet; build tokens and style rules on top of the design StyleSheet instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Keep the StyleDerive linking the StyleSheet to the BaseStyleSheet; removing it makes unstyled GuiObjects get odd defaults such as Size 0,0,0,0.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Create tokens early (colors, fonts, UDim sizes, text sizes) and link properties to them; tokens are highly recommended for updating UI styles over time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Use tag rules (.ButtonPrimary) with CollectionService tags to style specific UI objects without affecting every instance of a class.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Use built-in ViewportDisplaySize queries (Small/Medium/Large) and test with the Device Emulator across PS5, Average Laptop, and iPhone XR presets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Use Controller Emulator to verify PreferredInput-based style switches, such as gamepad layouts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Use a default TweenInfo transition on TextButton plus Hover GuiState rules to get smooth hover feedback with minimal scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): Create themes by duplicating a base theme and swapping only the theme tokens that differ, so both themes share a common token set.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/editor): When linking theme tokens, check the hover text for '$[Token] in ThemeA' to confirm the value comes from the theme, not the global token sheet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/input-action-system): Give every InputAction a binding for gamepad, keyboard/mouse, and touch so the game works cross-platform.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/input-action-system): Use InputActionLabel (beta) for on-screen control hints; it updates automatically on device switch or rebinding, with no scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/input-action-system): Use InputAction.PreferredBinding to build custom binding hints when you need full control over UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/missions): Enable SingleTaskMode in Constants to show a progress bar on missions with only one task.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/missions): Keep the Missions UI object hierarchy intact; moving objects breaks the code that finds them unless you update MissionsUI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/missions): Put translatable strings in FeaturePackagesCore TranslationStrings rather than hardcoding them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): Use Frames as containers for responsive list and flex layouts so child items adapt when the frame's size changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): Make a thin, long Frame to serve as a visual separator line between UI elements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): Clipping is on by default for GuiObject containers; set ClipsDescendants to false only when children must render outside the bounds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): Set StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation to true if clipping must work on rotated descendants (non-zero Rotation) inside a container.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): Clipping by a rotated container or by UICorner rounded corners is not supported; use CanvasGroup when you need clipping on rotated or rounded containers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/frames): For auto-sized frames, set Size to the minimum width and/or height, then set AutomaticSize to X, Y, or XY so the frame grows with its content.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/standout-games): Push visual boundaries (hyper-realistic 3D, stylized 2.5D sprites, high-fidelity avatars) so players think 'Wait, that's Roblox?'
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Before designing, list the questions a player has on join (goal, team, blaster, cooldown, etc.) and group UI elements by functional category.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Limit your color theme to only the key information colors should signal; players then associate colors with function quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Don't rely on color alone; pair colors with icons, shapes, or animations so colorblind players can still understand the UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Prioritize legibility over aesthetics; use simple UI with colors readable over both light and dark 3D backgrounds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Keep icons simple with limited detail so they stay recognizable on small mobile screens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Make the primary action the largest element, the secondary smaller, and tertiary subtle, matching how likely each action is.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Give interactable UI elements at least one form of visual feedback, such as outline, size, color, or animation when in focus.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Use clear, concise, distinct labels for interactive elements so players don't misread which action does what.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Test interactive element sizes across screen sizes; use UIAspectRatioConstraint to keep proportions on any device.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Aim for mobile touch zones of at least 9x9 mm, per WCAG touch target size and spacing guidance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Display UI text on a contrasting color or with a stroke so it isn't lost against the background.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Scale text per device and test on multiple devices, since text can become too large or unreadable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/choose-an-art-style): Use stylized fonts sparingly, such as for titles or alert text, since they are hard to read in large amounts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio): Pair UI interaction sounds with visual feedback (tween position, tint) so the state is clear for players with visual impairments and multiple senses are covered.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/chat-window): Channel tabs are disabled by default; enable ChannelTabsConfiguration explicitly if you want per-channel tabs (RBXGeneral and RBXSystem merge into one General tab).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/chat-window): Customize chat via ChatWindowMessageProperties and OnChatWindowAdded callbacks rather than overriding the existing UI, so default chat keeps working.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/chat-window): Reference system messages by TextChatMessage.Metadata rather than text, since default system messages are localized.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/chat-window): Use DeriveNewMessageProperties() from the chat window configuration as the base for per-message styling to keep defaults consistent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Show input-specific hints (1-5 for keyboard, gamepad button glyphs) based on the current primary input. Use InputActionLabel for no-code hints that update automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Position and size UI with UDim.Scale plus AnchorPoint rather than pixel offsets, since pixel sizes vary widely across devices and densities.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Use ViewportDisplaySize (Small/Medium/Large) and Style Queries to adapt layout; a 75% width container that fits a phone would be huge on a 4K TV.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Use UIListLayout and flex for containers so children reflow when siblings are added or removed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Keep all controls unobscured by device cutouts and Roblox UI, and add text size constraints so text stays legible on small and TV screens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/cross-platform): Don't rely on color alone to signal an action or outcome; ensure sufficient contrast.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/global-wind): Reduce Motion setting slows animated grass speed (not direction) for players who enable it; account for this in accessibility design.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/post-processing-effects): Use BlurEffect on the Camera to blur the 3D world behind open menus so players focus on UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/create-leaderboard): Clone a hidden template label for each row and use UIListLayout with LayoutOrder to keep leaderboard rows in order.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects): Use device emulation to check mobile control behaviour and how on-screen UI displays across different screen sizes and aspect ratios.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-input): Use PlaceholderText on a TextBox to tell players what input is expected.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-input): If a SurfaceGui TextBox is not visible, try a different face in the SurfaceGui's Face property.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-input): Restrict input format with GetPropertyChangedSignal('Text') and string.gsub, e.g. gsub(text, '%D', '') to allow only digits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware): Test touch targets, gesture conflicts and thumbstick/button reach on real phone screens, not emulated resolutions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware): Check text size, icon clarity and contrast on your lowest-supported device at arm's-length viewing distance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/plant-reference-project): Organise UI as Layers (top-level singletons) and Components (reusable clones of ReplicatedStorage templates); register layers with a central handler that controls visibility.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/plant-reference-project): Classify menus as HUD or Menu; show HUD only when no Menu is open and keep open Menus in a stack so closing a sub-menu returns to the previous one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/plant-reference-project): Imperative UI is quick to build visually in Studio and simple to change, but state desync bugs grow with complexity; declarative libraries (Roact, Fusion) trade editing ease for easier debugging.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/sound/objects): Play a Sound on GuiButton.Activated for auditory feedback on button presses; preload with Loaded:Wait() before playing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Add a UISizeConstraint with MinSize (e.g. 0, 40) so UI frames never shrink below a legible pixel height on small screens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Add a UIListLayout (Horizontal, VerticalAlignment Center) to frames so child GuiObjects arrange inside the frame instead of spilling outside its perimeter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Use SizeConstraint RelativeYY on icons and text labels so they scale with frame height and keep their aspect ratio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Use large, high-contrast text (e.g. TextSize 30 on a semi-transparent dark background) so it stays readable over busy 3D scenes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Set TextXAlignment to Left so score text stays anchored next to the icon as the number grows from 0 to 1,000,000.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-a-score-bar): Keep HUD elements from taking too much room; oversized UI can block the 3D view on small phone screens.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/become-an-artist): Keep only the Explorer and Properties windows open in Studio for a clearer workspace; reopen others later from the Window menu.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): To reverse order in a list, negate LayoutOrder values (0,1,2,3 becomes 0,-1,-2,-3) for right-to-left horizontal lists.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): Use UIListLayout.HorizontalFlex for tab bars so tabs fill width equally and auto-adjust when tab count changes, with no fixed widths.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): Use ItemLineAlignment = Stretch to make inconsistently sized tiles fill their row for a more cohesive layout.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): Use Padding for space between list items; use UIPadding for space around the list's outer bounds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): Prefer a UIGridLayout when items must strictly align to a consistent cell size in both X and Y; flex allows variable item counts per line.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/list-flex-layouts): For a single flexible element (e.g. slider bar between fixed labels), add a UIFlexItem child and set FlexMode to Fill, Grow, Shrink or Custom.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/minimap-system): Provide a toggle between minimap and world map: M key on keyboard, Up on gamepad DPad, and tapping the minimap on touchscreens, so all input types can reach the full map.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/minimap-system): Tune minimap_width/height and minimap_zoom separately from worldmap_width/height/zoom so the small corner map and full map each fit the screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/in-experience-asset-creation): Call PromptCreatePlatformContentAsync from a server-side script only; the Submit dialog (name/description) is Roblox-provided UI, so no custom save UI is needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/friends-locator): To replace the default locator UI, build a disabled ScreenGui with a FriendLocator frame; optionally name children Portrait and DisplayName so the module fills them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/player-data-purchasing): Replicate load and save error states to the client so it can disable purchase prompts and show warning dialogs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/input-and-camera/detect-user-input): Pass true as the third BindAction argument to auto-create a touchscreen button for mobile players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Define colors, sizes and fonts as tokens in one StyleSheet, then reference them from theme and rule sheets so changes propagate everywhere.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Keep themes with identical token sets; related themes must define the same tokens to swap correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Only one StyleSheet can apply to a given GUI tree; link the design sheet to the ScreenGui with a StyleLink.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Use tag selectors (.ClassName) with CollectionService to style groups of unrelated buttons consistently.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Use state selectors such as Enum.GuiState.Hover for interaction feedback instead of scripting hover visuals.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Use query selectors (@ prefix) with StyleQuery to adapt layouts to screen size, input type, or accessibility settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling): Use Reset to Default on bold (overridden) properties in Properties window to restore styled values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/scavenger-hunt): With useCustomModals, always provide a way to close custom modals or an automatic dismissal after a delay.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/scavenger-hunt): Use hideOtherGuis and showOtherGuis callbacks to keep specific ScreenGuis visible when modals appear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/scavenger-hunt): Default autoDismissTime is 20 seconds; set to 0 to disable auto-dismiss.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/foundational-gameplay-systems): Disable the default streaming-pause warning UI when it would break immersion, and replace it with your own transition.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-3D-audio): Use character dialogue audio in volumes near the spawn area to guide new players toward the objective.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/merch-booth): Use proximity prompts over in-game objects as an alternative to the 2D catalog so players discover items in the 3D world and try them on.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/merch-booth): Remove the default catalog button with toggleCatalogButton(false) when using custom buttons, shop regions or proximity prompts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/merch-booth): Set disableCharacterMovement = true while the booth is open if you want the character to stay still in a shop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): Warning: with platform-integrated chat enabled, custom UIs can no longer render message text themselves. Use TextChannelWindow instead of reading TextChatMessage.Text or MessageReceived for display.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): Clear the input TextBox after sending, since players expect the input to reset after pressing Enter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): Use a UIListLayout inside a ScrollingFrame with AutomaticSize XY on labels so chat messages stack and resize automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): Set ChatWindowConfiguration.Enabled and ChatInputBarConfiguration.Enabled to false to disable the default chat UI before building a custom one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/simple-custom-frontend-ui): Shade outgoing messages differently (BackgroundTransparency 0.5 while Status is Sending) so players can tell their pending messages apart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/homestore): Unpublished places show Roblox-created reference items in mannequins and shop; the catalog only shows the owner's items once published.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Set ScreenGui.ScreenInsets to DeviceSafeInsets to tuck HUD against the screen edge; CoreUISafeInsets leaves a large gap at the top.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Position and size HUD elements with Scale values so they adapt across resolutions; use Offset only for fixed pixel details like padding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Use UISizeConstraint (e.g. MaxSize inf,20) to stop bars becoming too tall on tablets while keeping width responsive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Use UICorner CornerRadius 0.5,0 for pill-shaped bars and UIStroke plus a semi-transparent dark fill so the bar reads on light and dark backgrounds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Set ImageLabel ZIndex higher than sibling frames so icons render in front of the meter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/9-slice): Set ScaleType to Slice and use Studio's 9-Slice Editor (SliceCenter ... button) to define borders visually instead of typing values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/9-slice): Use 9-slice for borders and corners on UI elements that must render at varying sizes or hold unknown-length localized text without distortion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/9-slice): Enter exact offset values in the editor's side fields for better precision than dragging the red dragger lines.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/9-slice): Remember editor offsets are pixel distances from image edges to draggers, not the UI object's SliceCenter values; don't copy them directly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Use Enum.AutomaticSize Y or XY on ScrollingFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize so scrolling appears automatically when child content overflows Size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): CanvasPosition does nothing unless scroll bars are visible; ensure canvas exceeds frame size before setting it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Set VerticalScrollBarInset to Always or ScrollBar so the canvas meets the scroll bar edge-to-edge instead of hiding content under it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Horizontal scroll bars can only sit below the canvas; only vertical bars can switch to left or right via VerticalScrollBarPosition.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Set StarterGui.ClipsDescendantsSupportsRotation to true if rotated descendants must be clipped by their container.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Clipping by a rotated container or by UICorner is unsupported; use CanvasGroup for rotated or rounded clipping.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): Set ElasticBehavior to Never on touch devices if the canvas must never scroll past its limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/scrolling-frames): ScrollingFrame suits displaying lots of information in a confined space and pairs well with list and grid layouts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/create-teams): Show team identity in multiple places: a custom on-screen player indicator, an in-game team indicator, the default leaderboard grouping, and name tints.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-inspect-menu): Disable the default profile-based inspect menu with GuiService:SetInspectMenuEnabled(false) when the profile outfit may not match the player's current in-game appearance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-inspect-menu): Use GuiService:InspectPlayerFromHumanoidDescription with humanoid:GetAppliedDescription() to show a player's currently equipped items rather than their saved Roblox profile outfit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-filtering): Use GetNonChatStringForUserAsync when showing filtered text to one specific user, since it depends on that user's age.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): Define OnIncomingMessage, OnBubbleAdded and OnChatWindowAdded on the client only; use them to style chat independently of each other.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/in-experience-text-chat): Disable ChatWindowConfiguration, ChatInputBarConfiguration, ChannelTabsConfiguration or BubbleChatConfiguration to hide their UI, or replace them with custom interfaces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Use a full-screen invisible back button so players can click or tap anywhere to close overlays, avoiding distracting buttons across PC, console and mobile.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Use Scale-based positioning and TextScaled inside a Frame so overlay text and images rescale across devices.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Reuse one ScreenGui/TextLabel (e.g., StoryNote) and inject text and RichText via script instead of creating many ScreenGuis per event.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative): Use RichText markup to style narration by speaker (color, font, size) and to interrupt sentences mid-line for entity intrusions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): Use ViewportFrame for item previews in inventory menus, equipped-item rotation, and minimaps instead of separate rendering setups.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): For a static preview, duplicate the top-level Camera and assign the duplicate to CurrentCamera so the game camera doesn't move the object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): When updating a ViewportFrame view, move the camera, not the objects inside the viewport.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/buying-upgrades): For SurfaceGUI sign text, set TextScaled on, use 0.5 scale and 0.5 anchor/position to center the label, and include the price in the text.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/buying-upgrades): Set ClickDetector MaxActivationDistance on buttons so players can interact from a sensible distance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive): Build lore into the playable area through UI features so players discover story while exploring.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Keep work in UpdateInput minimal; push larger computations to later steps. Reduce tween counts and keep TweenService callbacks light.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Avoid frequently changing parent or ZIndex of UI elements in LayerCollectors; keep element counts low before first render. Use fixed sizes for BillboardGuis.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/page-layouts): Use UIPageLayout for tabbed modals, tutorials, and character customization screens; each sibling GuiObject becomes a page.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/page-layouts): Drive page changes with UIPageLayout:Next() and Previous() from a client-side script in the layout's container.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Wireframe layouts before building in Studio so you can find pain points and fix designs before investing time in them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Group UI elements from the same category together so players know where to look for what they need.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Display UI contextually per workflow instead of showing too many categories at once to avoid clutter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Use art style to visually emphasise your intended interaction order when many UI elements compete for attention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Disable unneeded Roblox core UI (backpack, etc.) to free screen space for custom UI when gameplay doesn't need it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Block in UI with basic grayscale shapes so you can iterate quickly and keep the eye on information, not background colour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Design one adaptive layout that accounts for core UI and touch controls rather than maintaining separate UI versions per device.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Reserve space for mobile touch controls (virtual thumbstick bottom-left, jump bottom-right) even on PC layouts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Place interactive elements in thumb-reachable zones; areas far from bottom corners are uncomfortable or impossible on mobile and tablet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Test layouts against varied 3D backgrounds to keep on-screen UI clear and legible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Test layouts on mobile, tablet, laptop and TV screen sizes that players actually use.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Build user flows covering every player action, including quitting mid-round, leaving while joining, and brief disconnects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Test layouts with multiple player types, since what developers want players to do often differs from what players want to do.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Limit each flow chart to one primary task to keep it readable and actionable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts): Create separate user flows per input device (touch, gamepad, mouse) to find device-specific pain points.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/loading-screens): Set IgnoreGuiInset = true on the loading ScreenGui so it fills the full screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/loading-screens): Animate loading elements with TweenService (fade-in, looping rotation) to show that loading is progressing.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-2d-isometric-and-top-down-games-with-orthographic-camera/4966772): Use OrthographicMode Vertical for most games so characters are the same size on screen on every device; use Horizontal for side-scrollers.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4966810): Use the Input Action Manager (Window > Input > Input Action Manager) to build and audit cross-platform control mappings.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport): Use TeleportService:SetTeleportGui() on the client to replace the default loading screen with a custom ScreenGui; scripts inside it do not run.
